说来有趣，曾有一度，有人提出这些剧作的作者是一位英国女子。事实上，最早使它们在英国受到注意的英国学者劳伦斯·汉弗莱，就曾力图证明罗斯维塔不是别人，正是诺森布里亚的圣希尔达。他的说法当然站不住脚；然而，人们如此急切地想把这位才华横溢的女诗人、女剧作家认作本国之人，这件事本身就证明：在 16 世纪，她的作品备受推崇——正是在那个世纪，劳伦斯·汉弗莱因宗教信仰流亡异国，在德意志得识这些作品。如今已是确定无疑的事实：这些剧本出自萨克森甘德斯海姆的一位本笃会修女之手，而它们的优点也确实当得起她的传记作者那声赞叹：「Rara avis in Saxonia visa est」（萨克森大地上惊现一只珍禽）。

人们过去一向认定，从 6 世纪到 12 世纪，一切戏剧演出俱已断绝；然而只要耐心搜求，这些世纪中的每一个都交出过若干戏剧文本。封建时代被目为最野蛮的时代，日耳曼尼亚在当时——一如后世史册所载——被贬为最不开化的国度；然而恰恰是它们，产出了这批文本中最可观、最少残缺的作品，即罗斯维塔（Hrotsuitha，或作 Roswitha）的剧作——她是圣本笃会的一位修女，在甘德斯海姆修女院度过了她的修道生涯。

===Original===
There is a marked difference between her plays and such dramas as *The Mystery of the Wise and Foolish Virgins*, which is little more than an amplification of the sequence of the liturgy. We find here an author familiar not only with the Scriptures, the works of the Fathers of the Church, of the hagiographers, and of the Christian philosophers, but with Plautus, Terence, Horace, Virgil, and Ovid﻿—an author who, on her own confession, took the theatre of Terence as her model.

The Abbey of Gandersheim, where these plays were written, was founded about the year 850 by Ludolph, Duke of Saxony, at the request of his wife Oda, a Frankish princess. Although these were what men call “the dark ages,” the darkness was comparative. The Saxon court at this time was enlightened, and the Abbeys of Saxony, notably that of Corbei, were centres of learning and civilization. Gandersheim was one of the “free abbeys,” that is to say its Abbess held it direct from the King. Her rights of overlordship extended for many miles; she had her own law courts, and sent her men-at-arms into the field. In fact, she enjoyed the usual privileges and undertook the usual responsibilities of a feudal baron, and as such had the right to a seat in the Imperial Diet. Coins are extant, struck by the Abbesses of Gandersheim, whose portraits they bear.

During the 10th and 11th centuries these Abbesses were drawn chiefly from the royal house of Saxony, which had been raised to the dignity of the Imperial throne of Germania. Leuckfeld, in his voluminous history of Gandersheim, quotes a contemporary chronicler who praises the royal nuns for keeping all luxury and state out of the life of the community, and for observing the Rule of St. Benedict strictly. “They were forbidden,” says the chronicler, “to eat away from the common table at the appointed times, except in case of sickness. They slept together, and came together to celebrate the canonical hours. And they set to work together whenever work had to be done.” The Abbess who ruled the community in Roswitha’s time was Gerberg, or Gerberga, a niece of the Emperor Otho I. Gerberg was a good classical scholar, and Roswitha tells us, in one of the introductory prefaces with which, fortunately for posterity, her works are freely sprinkled, how much she owed to the tuition of this Abbess, “younger in years than I, but far older in learning.”

===Chinese===
她的剧本与《聪明童女与愚拙童女神秘剧》（The Mystery of the Wise and Foolish Virgins）一类戏剧判然有别——后者不过是礼拜仪式序列的铺陈扩充罢了。我们在她这里看到的是这样一位作者：不仅谙熟圣经、教父著作、圣徒传记作家与基督教哲学家们的作品，而且熟稔普劳图斯、泰伦提乌斯、贺拉斯、维吉尔与奥维德——而且，照她自己的招认，她正是以泰伦提乌斯的戏剧为自己的范本。

这些剧本的诞生地甘德斯海姆修道院，约于 850 年由萨克森公爵卢道夫应其妻、法兰克公主奥达之请而创建。虽然那时正值世人所谓的「黑暗时代」，但这黑暗只是相对而言。当时的萨克森宫廷开明有识，萨克森诸修道院——尤其是科尔维修道院——皆是学问与文明的中心。甘德斯海姆是「自由修道院」之一，也就是说，其院长修女直辖于国王。她的领主权力延绵数里；她有自己的法庭，也遣自己的武装扈从上阵。事实上，她享有封建男爵惯常享有的种种特权，也承担封建男爵惯常承担的种种义务，并且凭此身份享有帝国议会的席位。甘德斯海姆历代院长修女打铸的钱币至今犹存，币上还带着她们的肖像。

在 10 与 11 世纪，这些院长修女主要出自萨克森王族——彼时这一王族已登上日耳曼尼亚帝位的尊荣。洛伊克费尔德在其卷帙浩繁的甘德斯海姆史中，引述了一位同时代编年史家的话，赞扬这些王室修女将一切奢华与排场摈于修会生活之外，并严格遵守圣本笃会规。编年史家写道：「除患病外，她们不得在规定时刻离开公共餐桌另行进食。她们同寝共息，同聚诵念时辰日课；凡有工役，便一同劳作。」罗斯维塔时代治理这座修女院的院长，是格贝格，或作格贝加（Gerberga），皇帝奥托一世之侄女。格贝格是一位造诣颇深的古典学者。罗斯维塔的作品里点缀着大量序引文字——这是后世之大幸——她在其中一篇里告诉我们，她从这位院长的教诲中受益何其之多：她「年纪比我轻，学问却远比我长」。

===Original===
It is from such sentences as this that we are able to gain a little information about Roswitha’s life. Her mention of certain historical events and personages proves that she was born after the year 912 and before the year 940 (the known date of Gerberg’s birth). She seems to have entered the religious life at Gandersheim when she was about twenty-three years old. She tells us nothing about her antecedents, but as Gandersheim was an exclusive house we may assume that she was of gentle birth. What education or experience of the world she had had before she became a nun is a matter of guesswork.

Roswitha wrote in Latin, the only language used in the 10th century in the West for literary composition. Conrad Celtes, the well-known humanist, discovered the manuscript, the writing of which cannot be earlier than the 9th, or later than the 10th century, in the library of the Benedictine monastery of St. Emmeran, Ratisbon, in the last days of the 15th century. In the year 1501 it was printed. This first edition has an interesting frontispiece representing the nun poet and dramatist presenting her works to the Emperor Otho II, in the presence of her Abbess Gerberg, who wears the crown of a “Fürstabtin.” This and the other plates illustrating incidents in the plays have been attributed to both Dürer and Cranach, but they are not signed. Another edition, that of Schurzfleisch, in nearly all respects a reprint of the first, was issued in 1707, augmented with biographical and philological notes. The text given in the Latin Patrology (Migne, Tomus 137) is taken from the Schurzfleisch edition. More valuable to the student is Magnin’s edition. The French commentator collated the Celtes and Schurzfleisch texts with the original manuscript, which in 1803 had been moved from St. Emmeran to the Munich library, and found one or two readings preferable to those of Celtes. Magnin also restored some stage directions omitted by Celtes, one of which, in the eighth scene of *Callimachus*, affords, as the English translator notes, valuable evidence that the play was acted, or at least intended for representation.

The original manuscript is divided into three parts. The first contains eight poems or metrical legends of the Saints in which reliable authorities are carefully followed, much skill being shown, however, in the arrangement of the material and in the handling of the “leonine hexameter.” The second part consists of the six plays here given in English; the third, of a long unfinished poem called “Panegyric of the Othos.” Celtes changed the order, which is to be regretted, as it is obviously chronological. Roswitha’s preface to Part III shows more confidence than the preface to the plays, and very much more than the diffident preface to the poems. One of these poems, “Passio Sancti Pelagii,” once enjoyed a very high reputation, and is often quoted by Spanish and Portuguese hagiographers. The Bollandists print it entire in the *Acta Sanctorum*. It has another interest in that Roswitha tells us that she obtained her facts from a witness of the saint’s martyrdom.

===Chinese===
正是从这样的语句里，我们才得以获知一点关于罗斯维塔生平的讯息。她提及的某些史事与人物证明，她生于 912 年之后、940 年（格贝格已知的生年）之前。她似乎是在二十三岁上下进入甘德斯海姆开始修道生活的。对于自己的家世，她只字未提；不过，既然甘德斯海姆是一所门第森严的修女院，我们不妨推断她出身于高贵门第。至于她在成为修女之前受过什么教育、有过怎样的阅历，那就只能凭人猜度了。

罗斯维塔以拉丁文写作——在 10 世纪的西方，这是文学创作所用的唯一语言。15 世纪末，知名人文主义者康拉德·策尔蒂斯在雷根斯堡圣埃默兰本笃会修道院的藏书室里发现了这部手稿；其抄写年代不会早于 9 世纪，也不会晚于 10 世纪。1501 年，它付梓刊行。这第一版有一帧饶有趣味的卷首插画，画中这位修女诗人兼剧作家正将作品呈献给皇帝奥托二世，她的院长格贝格侍立一旁，头戴「帝国女院长」（Fürstabtin）之冠。这一幅以及描绘剧中情事的其余诸图，曾被归于丢勒与克拉纳赫两人名下，但均无签名。另一种版本，即舒尔茨弗莱施版，于 1707 年问世，几乎在所有方面都是第一版的重印，只是增补了传记与语文学的注释。《拉丁教父文集》（米涅，第 137 卷）所载文本即取自舒尔茨弗莱施版。对研究者更有价值的是马尼南的版本。这位法国评注家将策尔蒂斯本与舒尔茨弗莱施本同原始手稿进行了校勘——那部手稿已于 1803 年从圣埃默兰移藏慕尼黑图书馆——并发现一两处读法优于策尔蒂斯本。马尼南还恢复了若干被策尔蒂斯略去的舞台提示，其中《卡利马科斯》第八场的一条，恰如英译者所指出的，提供了宝贵的证据，证明该剧曾上演过，或至少是打算演出的。

原始手稿分为三个部分。第一部分收诗八首，即圣徒的韵体传奇，其中小心翼翼地依循可靠的权威史料，但在材料的布局与「列奥尼体六音步」（leonine hexameter）的驾驭上，颇见功力。第二部分即此处译为英文的六部剧作；第三部分是一首未完成的长诗，题为《奥托诸帝颂》（Panegyric of the Othos）。策尔蒂斯变更了原有的顺序，这是很可惋惜的，因为那顺序显然是按年代先后编排的。罗斯维塔为第三部分所写的序言，比剧作的序言显出更多的自信，更远远胜过那篇怯生生的小诗序言。这些诗中有一首《圣佩拉吉乌斯受难记》（Passio Sancti Pelagii），一度享有极高的声誉，西班牙与葡萄牙的圣徒传记作家常加征引。博兰德学会将全文收入《圣徒行传》（Acta Sanctorum）刊行。它还有另一重意味：罗斯维塔告诉我们，她的事实材料得自这位圣徒殉道的目击者。

===Original===
Although Roswitha claims Terence as her master in the art of playwriting, it cannot be said that she imitates him closely. When *Paphnutius* was acted in London in 1914 the dramatic critic of *The Times* was justified from one point of view in asserting that Roswitha’s style is “not in the least Terentian.” For one thing she is quite indifferent to the “unities,” and transports us from place to place with bewildering abruptness. Her relation to Terence, as she herself insists, is one of moral contrasts rather than of literary parallels. The “situation” in Terence’s comedies almost invariably turns on the frailty of women; in Roswitha’s plays as invariably on their heroic adherence to chastity. Although considerable variety is shown in the treatment of each story, the motive is always the same﻿—to glorify uncompromising fidelity to the vow of virginity. This nun dramatist deals courageously, but, it must be added, delicately, when it is remembered that she lived in an age when even the best educated were neither fastidious nor restrained in manners or conversation, with the temptations which her characters overcome. The preface to her plays shows that it was not without some qualms of conscience that she wrote of things “which should not even be named among us.” But the purity of her intentions, which was obviously recognized by her religious superiors, should induce the most prudish reader to refrain from charges of impropriety. With all their shortcomings, Roswitha’s works have a claim to an eminent place in medieval literature, and do honour to her sex, to the age in which she lived, and to the vocation which she followed.

===Chinese===
罗斯维塔虽奉泰伦提乌斯为编剧艺术之师，却不能说她亦步亦趋地模仿他。1914 年《帕夫努提乌斯》在伦敦上演时，《泰晤士报》的戏剧评论家断言罗斯维塔的风格「丝毫没有泰伦提乌斯的气息」，从一个角度看，这话并非无据。其一，她对「三一律」全然无所顾惜，带我们辗转于一个个地点之间，其仓促突兀令人目眩。她与泰伦提乌斯的关系，正如她自己坚称的，是道德上的对照，而非文学上的因袭。泰伦提乌斯喜剧中的「关目」几乎无一不以女子的脆弱为枢轴；罗斯维塔剧中的关目则无一不以女子对贞洁的英勇坚守为枢轴。各剧的情节处理虽变化多端，动机却始终如一——颂扬对守贞誓言毫不妥协的忠诚。这位修女剧作家勇敢地处理她的角色所战胜的种种诱惑，但必须补充一句：她处理得极有分寸——要记得，她生活的那个时代，连教养最好的人士在举止谈吐上也谈不上讲究与克制。她的剧作序言表明，她提笔写那些「在我们中间连提都不该提」的事情时，并非全无良知的忐忑。然而她动机的纯洁——这一点显然得到了她修道长上的认可——当能令最为拘谨的读者也不再指其失当。纵有种种缺憾，罗斯维塔的作品在中世纪文学中理应占有一个显赫的位置，并为她的性别、为她所处的时代、为她所奉的圣召增光。

## Paphnutius / 帕夫努提乌斯

### Argument / 剧情提要

===Original===
The conversion of Thais by the hermit Paphnutius. Obedient to a vision, he leaves the desert, and, disguised as a lover, seeks out Thais in Alexandria. She is moved to repent by his exhortations and, renouncing her evil life, consents to be enclosed in a narrow cell, where she does penance for three years. Paphnutius learns from a vision granted to Anthony's disciple Paul that her humility has won her a place among the blessed in Paradise. He brings her out of her cell and stays by her side until her soul has left her body.

===Chinese===
隐修士帕夫努提乌斯（Paphnutius）劝化泰绮丝（Thais）皈依。他遵异象之命离开沙漠，乔装成情人，到亚历山大里亚寻访泰绮丝。她受他劝诫感动而悔悟，弃绝恶浊生涯，同意被幽禁于一间窄小的斗室，在其中补赎三年。帕夫努提乌斯从安东尼（Antony）的弟子保罗（Paul）所得的异象得知，她的谦卑已为她赢得乐园中诸福者的席位。他把她带出斗室，守在她身旁，直到她的灵魂离开身体。

### Characters / 人物表

===Original===
- Paphnutius
- Thais
- The Abbess
- Lovers fo Thais
- Disciples of Paphnutius
- Antony
- Paul

===Chinese===
- 帕夫努提乌斯
- 泰绮丝
- 女修道院院长
- 泰绮丝的情人们
- 帕夫努提乌斯的众弟子
- 安东尼
- 保罗

### Paphnutius / 帕夫努提乌斯

#### Scene I / 第一场

===Original===
Disciples9
Why do you look so gloomy, father Paphnutius? Why do you not smile at us as usual?

Paphnutius
When the heart is sad the face clouds over. It is only natural.

Disciples
But why are you sad?

Paphnutius
I grieve over an injury to my Creator.

Disciples
What injury?

===Chinese===
众弟子9
帕夫努提乌斯神父，您为何神色如此阴郁？为何不像往常那样对我们微笑？

帕夫努提乌斯
心里忧伤，面容便阴沉。这是自然之理。

众弟子
可您为何忧伤呢？

帕夫努提乌斯
我为我的创造者所受的侵害而哀恸。

众弟子
什么侵害？

===Original===
Paphnutius
The injury His own creatures made in His very image inflict on Him.

Disciples
Oh, father, your words fill us with fear! How can such things be?

Paphnutius
It is true that the impassible Majesty cannot be hurt by injuries. Nevertheless, speaking in metaphor, and as if God were weak with our weakness, what greater injury can we conceive than this—that while the greater world is obedient, and subject to His rule, the lesser world resists His guidance?

Disciples
What do you mean by the lesser world?

Paphnutius
Man.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
就是祂按自己形象所造的受造之物，加于祂的侵害。

众弟子
啊，神父，您的话使我们满心恐惧！怎会有这样的事？

帕夫努提乌斯
诚然，那不能受苦的至尊并不会真的被伤害所损伤。然而，若以比喻言之，姑且把上帝看作同我们一样软弱，那么我们还能设想比这更大的侵害吗——大世界恭顺服从祂的统辖，小世界却抗拒祂的引领？

众弟子
您所说的小世界是什么意思？

帕夫努提乌斯
人。

===Original===
Disciples
Man?

Paphnutius
Yes.

Disciples
What man?

Paphnutius
Every man.

Disciples
How can this be?

===Chinese===
众弟子
人？

帕夫努提乌斯
正是。

众弟子
什么人？

帕夫努提乌斯
每一个人。

众弟子
这怎么可能？

===Original===
Paphnutius
It has pleased our Creator.

Disciples
We do not understand.

Paphnutius
It is not plain to many.

Disciples
Explain, father.

Paphnutius
Be attentive, then.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
我们的创造者如此乐意。

众弟子
我们不明白。

帕夫努提乌斯
这对许多人来说都不易懂。

众弟子
请讲解，神父。

帕夫努提乌斯
那就留心听。

===Original===
Disciples
We are eager to learn.

Paphnutius
You know that the greater world is composed of four elements which are contraries, yet by the will of the Creator these contraries are adjusted in harmonious arrangement. Now, man is composed of even more contrary parts.

Disciples
What can be more contrary than the elements?

Paphnutius
The body and the soul. The soul is not mortal like the body, nor the body spiritual as is the soul.

Disciples
That is true. But what did you mean, father, when you spoke of "harmonious arrangement"?

===Chinese===
众弟子
我们亟于受教。

帕夫努提乌斯
你们知道，大世界由四种相互对立的元素构成，然而凭着创造者的旨意，这些对立之物被调和成和谐的秩序。而人，则由更为对立的部分构成。

众弟子
还有什么比元素更对立的呢？

帕夫努提乌斯
身体与灵魂。灵魂不像身体那样有死，身体也不像灵魂那样属灵。

众弟子
这倒是真的。可是神父，您说「和谐的秩序」是什么意思？

===Original===
Paphnutius
I meant that as low and high sounds harmoniously united produce a certain music, so discordant elements rightly adjusted make one world.

Disciples
It seems strange that discords can become concords.

Paphnutius
Consider. No thing is composed of "likes"—neither can it be made up of elements which have no proportion among themselves, or which are entirely different in substance and nature.

Disciples
What is music, master?

Paphnutius
One of the branches of the "quadrivium" of philosophy, my son. Arithmetic, geometry, music, and philosophy form the quadrivium.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
我的意思是：正如低音与高音和谐相合便产生音乐，不和谐的元素经过恰当调和便构成一个世界。

众弟子
不谐竟能化为谐和，这似乎很奇怪。

帕夫努提乌斯
想想吧。没有一物是由「同类」构成的——它也不可能由彼此毫无比例、或实质与本性完全不同的元素构成。

众弟子
老师，什么是音乐？

帕夫努提乌斯
孩子，它是哲学「四艺」（quadrivium）的一支。算术、几何、音乐与哲学构成四艺。

===Original===
Disciples
I should like to know why they are given that name.

Paphnutius
Because just as paths branch out from the quadrivium, the place where four roads meet, so do these subjects lead like roads from one principle of philosophy.

Disciples
We had best not question you about the other three, for our slow wits can scarcely follow what you have told us about the first.

Paphnutius
It is a difficult subject.

Disciples
Still you might give us a general idea of the nature of music.

===Chinese===
众弟子
我想知道它们为何得此名。

帕夫努提乌斯
因为正如小径从四岔路口——四条道路交汇之处——分岔而出，这些学科也像道路一样，从哲学的同一个原理延展开去。

众弟子
我们最好还是不问其余三门了，因为以我们迟钝的悟性，就连您讲的第一门都勉强才跟得上。

帕夫努提乌斯
这是一门艰深的学问。

众弟子
不过您总可以给我们讲讲音乐本性的大概。

===Original===
Paphnutius
It is hard to explain to hermits to whom it is an unknown science.

Disciples
Is there more than one kind of music?

Paphnutius
There are three kinds, my son. The first is celestial, the second human, the third is produced by instruments.

Disciples
In what does the celestial consist?

Paphnutius
In the seven planets and the celestial globe.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
对隐修士来说，这是一门陌生的学问，很难讲解。

众弟子
音乐不止一种吗？

帕夫努提乌斯
孩子，有三种。第一种属天，第二种属人，第三种由乐器发出。

众弟子
属天的音乐由什么构成？

帕夫努提乌斯
由七颗行星与天球构成。

===Original===
Disciples
But how?

Paphnutius
Exactly as in instruments. You find the same number of intervals of the same length, and the same concords as in strings.

Disciples
We do not understand what intervals are.

Paphnutius
The dimensions which are reckoned between planets or between notes.

Disciples
And what are their lengths?

===Chinese===
众弟子
可是如何构成呢？

帕夫努提乌斯
与乐器中的道理一模一样。你会发现同样数目、同样长度的音程，以及与琴弦上相同的协和音。

众弟子
我们不明白什么是音程。

帕夫努提乌斯
就是行星之间或音符之间计量的间距。

众弟子
它们的长度又是多少？

===Original===
Paphnutius
The same as tones.

Disciples
We are none the wiser.

Paphnutius
A tone is composed of two sounds, and bears the ratio of nine to eight.

Disciples
As soon as we get over one difficulty, you place a greater one in our path!

Paphnutius
That is inevitable in a discussion of this kind.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
与全音相同。

众弟子
我们还是不懂。

帕夫努提乌斯
一个全音由两个音构成，其比例为九比八。

众弟子
我们刚迈过一道难关，您又在路上设下一道更大的！

帕夫努提乌斯
这类讨论在所难免。

===Original===
Disciples
Yet tell us something about concord, so that at least we may know the meaning of the word.

Paphnutius
Concord, harmony, or symphonia may be defined as a fitting disposition of modulation. It is composed sometimes of three, sometimes of four, sometimes of five sounds.

Disciples
As you have given us these three distinctions, we should like to learn the name of each.

Paphnutius
The first is called a fourth, as consisting of four sounds, and it has the proportion of four to three. The second is called a fifth. It consists of five sounds and bears the ratio of one and a half. The third is known as the diapason; it is double and is perfected in eight sounds.

===Chinese===
众弟子
那就给我们讲讲协和吧，好歹让我们知道这个词的含义。

帕夫努提乌斯
协和、和声，或称谐音（symphonia），可以定义为音调变化的恰当配置。它有时由三个音、有时由四个音、有时由五个音构成。

众弟子
您既给了我们这三种区分，我们想晓得它们各自的名字。

帕夫努提乌斯
第一种称为四度，因它由四个音构成，比例为四比三。第二种称为五度，由五个音构成，比例为一又二分之一。第三种称为八度（diapason）；它是倍律，在八个音中得以完成。

===Original===
Disciples
And do the spheres and planets produce sounds, since they are compared to notes?

Paphnutius
Undoubtedly they do.

Disciple
Why is the music not heard?

Disciples
Yes, why is it not heard?

Paphnutius
Many reasons are given. Some think it is not heard because it is so continuous that men have grown accustomed to it. Others say it is because of the density of the air. Some assert that so enormous a sound could not pass into the mortal ear. Others that the music of the spheres is so pleasant and sweet that if it were heard all men would come together, and, forgetting themselves and all their pursuits, would follow the sounds from east to west.

===Chinese===
众弟子
既然诸天球与行星被比作音符，它们也发出声音吗？

帕夫努提乌斯
无疑发出。

弟子
那为何听不见这音乐？

众弟子
是啊，为何听不见？

帕夫努提乌斯
说法很多。有人认为听不见，是因为它连绵不绝，人们早已习以为常。有人说是因空气稠密。有人断言如此浩大的声音不可能进入凡人的耳朵。还有人说，诸天球的音乐如此悦耳甘美，人若听见，便会齐聚一处，忘却自身与一切营求，追随那声音从东到西。

===Original===
Disciples
It is well that it is not heard.

Paphnutius
As our Creator foreknew.

Disciples
We have heard enough of this kind of music. What of "human" music?

Paphnutius
What do you want to know about that?

Disciples
How is it manifested?

Paphnutius
Not only, as I have already told you, in the combination of body and soul, and in the utterance of the voice, now high, now low, but even in the pulsation of the veins, and in the proportion of our members. Take the finger-joints. In them, if we measure, we find the same proportions as we have already found in concord; for music is said to be a fitting disposition not only of sounds, but of things with no resemblance to sounds.

===Chinese===
众弟子
幸而听不见。

帕夫努提乌斯
我们的创造者早有先见。

众弟子
这类音乐我们听够了。「属人」的音乐又如何？

帕夫努提乌斯
你们想知晓它的什么？

众弟子
它如何显明？

帕夫努提乌斯
如我方才所言，它不仅显明于身体与灵魂的结合、嗓音时高时低的发抒，甚至也显明于血脉的搏动、肢体间的比例。就拿手指关节来说，若加以度量，我们会发现与协和音中相同的比例；因为音乐据称不仅是声音的恰当配置，也是与声音毫无相似之物的恰当配置。

===Original===
Disciples
Had we known the difficulty that such a hard point presents to the ignorant, we would not have asked you about your "lesser world." It is better to know nothing than to be bewildered.

Paphnutius
I do not agree. By trying to understand you have learned many things that you did not know before.

Disciples
That is true.

Disciple
True it may be, but I am weary of this disputation. We are all weary, because we cannot follow the reasoning of such a philosopher!

Paphnutius
Why do you laugh at me, children? I am no philosopher, but an ignorant man.

===Chinese===
众弟子
早知道这样的难题让无知者如此为难，我们就不会问您那个「小世界」了。与其困惑迷惘，不如一无所知。

帕夫努提乌斯
我不以为然。你们因力求理解，已经学会了许多从前不知道的东西。

众弟子
这倒是真的。

弟子
或许是真的，可我对这场论辩已经厌倦了。我们都厌倦了，因为我们跟不上这样一位哲人的推理！

帕夫努提乌斯
孩子们，你们为何笑我？我不是哲人，不过是个无知的人。

===Original===
Disciples
Where did you get all this learning with which you have puzzled our heads?

Paphnutius
It is but a little drop from the full deep wells of learning—wells at which I, a chance passerby, have lapped, but never sat down to drain.

Disciple
We are grateful for your patience with us; but I for one cannot forget the warning of the Apostle: "God hath chosen the foolish things of the world to confound the wise."

Paphnutius
Whether a fool or a wise man does wrong, he will be confounded.

Disciples
True.

===Chinese===
众弟子
您这些把我们脑袋搅昏的学问，是从哪里得来的？

帕夫努提乌斯
那不过是学问深井中溢出的一滴——我这偶然的路人，曾在井边掬水而饮，却从未坐下来将井饮尽。

弟子
我们感激您对我们的耐心；但就我而言，使徒的告诫不敢忘怀：「上帝拣选了世上的愚拙，叫有智慧的羞愧。」

帕夫努提乌斯
无论愚人还是智者作恶，都必羞愧。

众弟子
不错。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Nor is God offended by Knowledge of the Knowable, only by undue pride on the part of the Knower.

Disciples
That is well said.

Paphnutius
And I would ask you—unto whose praise can the knowledge of the arts be more worthily or more justly turned than to the praise of Him Who made things capable of being known, and gave us the capacity to know them?

Disciples
Truly, to none.

Paphnutius
The more a man realizes the wonderful way in which God has set all things in number and measure and weight, the more ardent his love.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
上帝也不因人认识可认识之物而不悦，祂不悦的只是认识者的妄自尊大。

众弟子
说得好。

帕夫努提乌斯
我且问你们——诸艺之识，归于谁的赞美才更相称、更公正呢？岂不是归于那位使万物可被认识、又赐我们认识能力的祂吗？

众弟子
确实，不归于别的。

帕夫努提乌斯
人越是领悟上帝以数、以度、以衡安置万物的奇妙，他的爱便越发炽热。

===Original===
Disciples
That is as it should be.

Paphnutius
But I am wrong to dwell on matters which give you so little pleasure.

Disciples
Tell us the cause of your sadness. Relieve us of the burden of our curiosity.

Paphnutius
Perhaps you will not find the tale to your liking.

Disciples
A man is often sadder for having his curiosity satisfied, yet he cannot overcome this tendency to be curious. It is part of our weakness.

===Chinese===
众弟子
理当如此。

帕夫努提乌斯
可我不该在这些你们不甚欢喜的事上流连。

众弟子
告诉我们您忧伤的缘由吧。解除我们好奇心的重负。

帕夫努提乌斯
这故事你们或许不爱听。

众弟子
人的好奇心得到满足后往往更加忧愁，可他又克服不了这好奇的习性。这是我们的软弱之一。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Brothers—there is a woman, a shameless woman, living in our neighbourhood.

Disciples
A perilous thing for the people.

Paphnutius
Her beauty is wonderful: her impurity is—horrible.

Disciples
What is her wretched name?

Paphnutius
Thais.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
弟兄们——有一个女人，一个无耻的女人，住在我们附近。

众弟子
对众人是一件危险的事。

帕夫努提乌斯
她的美貌令人惊叹：她的污秽——骇人。

众弟子
她那可怜的名字是什么？

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝。

===Original===
Disciples
Thais! Thais, the harlot!

Paphnutius
Yes—she.

Disciple
Everyone has heard of her and her wickedness.

Paphnutius
It is no wonder, for she is not satisfied to ruin herself with a small band of lovers. She seeks to allure all men through her marvellous beauty, and drag them down with her.

Disciples
What a woeful thing!

Paphnutius
And it is not only fools and wastrels who squander their substance with her. Citizens of high standing and virtue lay precious things at her feet, and enrich her to their own undoing.

===Chinese===
众弟子
泰绮丝！那个妓女泰绮丝！

帕夫努提乌斯
正是——是她。

弟子
人人听说过她和她的恶行。

帕夫努提乌斯
这不足为奇，因为她并不满足于与一小伙情人一同毁掉自己。她还要以绝世美貌诱惑众生，把他们与她一同拖入深渊。

众弟子
何等可悲的事！

帕夫努提乌斯
在她身上挥霍家财的，还不止是蠢人和浪子。位高德劭的公民也把珍宝放在她脚下，供养她，以致自毁。

===Original===
Disciples
It is terrible to hear of such things.

Paphnutius
Flocks of lovers crowd to her doors.

Disciples
And to their destruction!

Paphnutius
They are so crazed with desire that they quarrel and fight for admission to her house.

Disciples
One vice brings another in its train.

===Chinese===
众弟子
听闻这样的事实在可怕。

帕夫努提乌斯
成群的情人拥挤在她的门前。

众弟子
也是挤向他们的毁灭！

帕夫努提乌斯
他们欲火中烧，为了争先进她的家门而争吵斗殴。

众弟子
一种恶行牵引着另一种。

===Original===
Paphnutius
They come to blows. Heads are broken, faces bruised, noses smashed; at times they drive each other out with weapons, and the threshold of the vile place is dyed with blood!

Disciples
Most horrible!

Paphnutius
This is the injury to the Creator for which I weep day and night. This is the cause of my sorrow.

Disciples
We understand now. You have good reason to be distressed, and I doubt not that the citizens of the heavenly country share your grief.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
他们大打出手。头颅打破，脸面青肿，鼻梁碎裂；有时他们持械相互驱逐，那肮脏处所的门槛竟被鲜血染红！

众弟子
骇人至极！

帕夫努提乌斯
这就是我昼夜为之哭泣的、对创造者的侵害。这便是我忧伤的缘由。

众弟子
我们如今明白了。您有充分的理由忧愁，而且我毫不怀疑，天国的公民们也分担着您的哀伤。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Oh, to rescue her from that wicked life! Why should I not try?

Disciples
God forbid!

Paphnutius
Brother, our Lord Jesus went among sinners.

Disciples
She would not receive a hermit.

Paphnutius
What if I were to go in the disguise of a lover?

Disciple
If that thought is from God, God will give you strength to accomplish it.

Paphnutius
I will set out immediately. I shall need your best prayers. Pray that I may not be overcome by the wiles of the serpent. Pray that I may be able to show this soul the beauty of divine love.

Disciple
May He Who laid low the Prince of Darkness give you the victory over the enemy of the human race.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
啊，要把她从那恶浊生涯中救出来！我为何不去一试？

众弟子
上帝不容！

帕夫努提乌斯
弟兄，我们的主耶稣曾与罪人相处。

众弟子
她不会接待一个隐修士。

帕夫努提乌斯
倘若我乔装成一个情人前去呢？

弟子
这念头若出于上帝，上帝必赐您力量成就它。

帕夫努提乌斯
我即刻启程。我需要你们恳切的祷告。求你们祈祷，愿我不致被蛇的诡计所胜；祈祷我能让这个灵魂看见神圣之爱的美。

弟子
愿那击倒黑暗之君的祂，赐您胜过人类之敌。

#### Scene II / 第二场

===Original===
Paphnutius
I am bewildered in this town. I cannot find my way. Now I shut my eyes, and I am back in the desert. I can hear my children's voices praising God. Good children, I know you are praying for me! I fear to speak. I fear to ask my way. O God, come to my help! I see some young men in the marketplace. They are coming this way. I will go up to them and ask where she is to be found.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
我在这城里晕头转向。找不到路。此刻我一闭上眼，便回到了沙漠。我能听见孩子们的歌声赞美上帝。好孩子们，我知道你们正在为我祈祷！我惧怕开口。我惧怕问路。上帝啊，求您救助我！我看见广场上有几个青年。他们正朝这边来。我要迎上去，问他们她在哪里。

===Original===
The Young Men
That stranger seems to want to speak to us.

Young Man
Let us go and find out.

Paphnutius
Your pardon, gentlemen. Am I speaking to citizens of this town?

Young Man
You are. Can we do anything for you?

Paphnutius
My salutations!

Young Man
And ours, whether you are a native or a foreigner.

===Chinese===
众青年
那个外乡人像是有话要对我们说。

青年
我们去问个明白。

帕夫努提乌斯
诸位，恕我冒昧。我是在同本城的公民说话吗？

青年
正是。我们能为您效劳吗？

帕夫努提乌斯
谨致问候！

青年
我们也致问候，无论您是本乡人还是外乡人。

===Original===
Paphnutius
I am a stranger.

Young Man
What brings you here? Have you come for pleasure, business, or learning? This is a great city for learning. Which is it?

Paphnutius
I cannot say.

Young Man
Why?

Paphnutius
That is my secret.

Young Man
It would be wiser to tell us your secret. It will be difficult for you, a stranger, to do your business here without the advice of us citizens.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
我是外乡人。

青年
您为何而来？是为了游乐、事务，还是求学？这是一座学术大城。究竟是哪一样？

帕夫努提乌斯
我说不好。

青年
为何？

帕夫努提乌斯
那是我的秘密。

青年
把秘密告诉我们才是明智的。您一个外乡人，没有我们本地人的指点，在此地办事务必为难。

===Original===
Paphnutius
But if I tell you, you may try to hinder me from carrying out my plans.

Young Man
You can trust us. We are men of honour!

Paphnutius
I believe it. I will trust in your loyalty and tell you my secret.

Young Man
We are not traitors. No harm shall come to you.

Paphnutius
I am told that there lives in this town a woman who loves all who love her. She is kind to all men; she'll not deny them anything.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
可我若告诉你们，你们也许会阻挠我行事。

青年
您尽可信任我们。我们是体面人！

帕夫努提乌斯
这我相信。我就信你们的忠义，把秘密相告。

青年
我们不是告密之徒。您不会受到任何伤害。

帕夫努提乌斯
我听说本城住着一个女人，凡爱她的她都爱。她对所有男人都和善；他们求什么她都不拒绝。

===Original===
Young Man
Stranger, you must tell us her name. There are many women of that kind in our city. Do you know her name?

Paphnutius
Yes, I know it.

Young Man
Who is she?

Paphnutius
Thais.

Young Man
Thais! She is the flame of this land! She sets all hearts on fire.

Paphnutius
They say she is beautiful. The most exquisite woman of her kind in the world!

===Chinese===
青年
外乡人，您得说出她的名字。我们城里这种女人多得很。您知道她的名字吗？

帕夫努提乌斯
是，我知道。

青年
是谁？

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝。

青年
泰绮丝！她是这片土地的火焰！她点燃所有的心。

帕夫努提乌斯
听说她很美。是世上她那一行里最出众的女人！

===Original===
Young Man
They have not deceived you.

Paphnutius
For her sake I have made a long and difficult journey. I have come here only to see her.

Young Man
Well, what should prevent you? You are young and handsome.

Paphnutius
Where does she live?

Young Man
Over there. Her house is quite near this place.

Paphnutius
That house?

Young Man
Yes, to the left of the statue.

===Chinese===
青年
他们没有骗您。

帕夫努提乌斯
为了她，我跋涉了漫长而艰辛的路途。我到此地只为见她一面。

青年
这有什么可拦阻您的？您又年轻又俊美。

帕夫努提乌斯
她住在哪里？

青年
就在那边。她的家离这儿很近。

帕夫努提乌斯
那座房子？

青年
对，雕像左边。

===Original===
Paphnutius
I will go there.

Young Man
If you like, we will come with you.

Paphnutius
I thank you for the courtesy, but I would rather go alone.

Young Man
We understand. Have you money in your purse, stranger? Thais loves a handsome face, but she loves a full purse more.

Paphnutius
Gentlemen, I am rich. I have a rare present to offer her.

Young Man
To our next meeting, then! Farewell. May Thais be kind!

Paphnutius
Farewell.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
我就去那里。

青年
您若愿意，我们陪您同去。

帕夫努提乌斯
多谢诸位好意，但我宁愿独行。

青年
我们明白。外乡人，您钱袋里有的是钱吗？泰绮丝爱俊俏的脸，但她更爱满满的钱袋。

帕夫努提乌斯
诸位，我很富有。我有一件稀世的礼物要送她。

青年
那就待下次相见！再会。愿泰绮丝善待您！

帕夫努提乌斯
再会。

#### Scene III / 第三场

===Original===
Paphnutius
Thais! Thais!

Thais
Who is there? I do not know that voice.

Paphnutius
Thais! Your lover speaks! Thais!

Thais
Stranger, who are you?

Paphnutius
Arise, my love, my beautiful one, and come!

Thais
Who are you?

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝！泰绮丝！

泰绮丝
是谁？这声音我不认识。

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝！你的情人在呼唤！泰绮丝！

泰绮丝
外乡人，你是谁？

帕夫努提乌斯
起来，我的爱人，我的美人，来吧！

泰绮丝
你是谁？

===Original===
Paphnutius
A man who loves you!

Thais
And what do you want with me?

Paphnutius
I will show you.

Thais
You would be my lover?

Paphnutius
I am your lover, Thais, flame of the world!

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
一个爱你的男人！

泰绮丝
你要我做什么？

帕夫努提乌斯
我会让你明白。

泰绮丝
你想做我的情人？

帕夫努提乌斯
我就是你的情人，泰绮丝，世上的火焰！

===Original===
Thais
Whoever loves me is well paid. He receives as much as he gives.

Paphnutius
Oh, Thais, Thais! If you knew what a long and troublesome journey I have come to speak to you—to see your face!

Thais
Well? Have I refused to speak to you, or to show you my face?

Paphnutius
I cannot speak to you here. I must be with you alone. What I have to say is secret. The room must be secret too.

Thais
How would you like a bedchamber, fragrant with perfumes, adorned as for a marriage? I have such a room. Look!

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
凡爱我的都得到丰厚的报偿。他得到的不亚于他付出的。

帕夫努提乌斯
啊，泰绮丝，泰绮丝！你若知道我走了多么漫长、多么劳顿的路途，只为同你说话——只为见你的容颜！

泰绮丝
那又如何？我可曾拒绝同你说话，或拒绝让你看我的脸？

帕夫努提乌斯
我不能在这里同你说话。我必须单独与你相处。要说的话是隐秘的。房间也必须是隐秘的。

泰绮丝
你想要一间香氛馥郁、装点得如婚房一般的寝室吗？我有这样一间屋子。看！

===Original===
Paphnutius
Is there no room still more secret—a room that your lovers do not know? Some room where you and I might hide from all the world?

Thais
Yes, there is a room like that in this house. No one even knows that it exists except myself, and God.

Paphnutius
God! What God?

Thais
The true God.

Paphnutius
You believe that He exists?

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
可还有更隐秘的房间吗——一间你的情人们都不知道的房间？一间你我可以在其中避开全世界的房间？

泰绮丝
有，这宅子里有一间这样的屋子。除了我自己，没有一个人知道它的存在——除了上帝。

帕夫努提乌斯
上帝！哪位上帝？

泰绮丝
真神。

帕夫努提乌斯
你相信祂存在？

===Original===
Thais
I am a Christian.

Paphnutius
And you believe that He knows what we do?

Thais
I believe He knows everything.

Paphnutius
What do you think, then? That He is indifferent to the actions of the sinner, or that He reserves judgment?

Thais
I suppose that the merits of each man are weighed in the balance, and that we shall be punished or rewarded according to our deeds.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
我是基督徒。

帕夫努提乌斯
你也相信祂知道我们的所作所为？

泰绮丝
我相信祂无所不知。

帕夫努提乌斯
那么你怎么想？你以为祂对罪人的行为无动于衷，还是以为祂把审判留待日后？

泰绮丝
我想每个人的功过都会放上天秤称量，我们将按自己的行为或受罚、或得赏。

===Original===
Paphnutius
O Christ! How wondrous is Thy patience! How wondrous is Thy love! Even when those who believe in Thee sin deliberately, Thou dost delay their destruction!

Thais
Why do you tremble? Why do you turn pale? Why do you weep?

Paphnutius
I shudder at your presumption. I weep for your damnation. How, knowing what you know, can you destroy men in this manner and ruin so many souls, all precious and immortal?

Thais
Your voice pierces my heart! Strange lover—you are cruel. Pity me!

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
基督啊！您的忍耐何等奇妙！您的爱何等奇妙！即使信您的人存心犯罪，您仍迟迟不施毁灭！

泰绮丝
你为何颤抖？为何面色发白？为何流泪？

帕夫努提乌斯
我为你的胆大妄为而战栗，为你的沉沦而哭泣。你既知道你所知道的，怎还能这样毁灭男人，败坏那么多灵魂——个个都是宝贵而不朽的？

泰绮丝
你的声音刺穿了我的心！奇怪的情人——你太残忍。怜悯我吧！

===Original===
Paphnutius
Let us pity rather those souls whom you have deprived of the sight of God—of the God Whom you confess! Oh, Thais, you have willfully offended the divine Majesty. That condemns you.

Thais
What do you mean? Why do you threaten me like this?

Paphnutius
Because the punishment of hellfire awaits you if you remain in sin.

Thais
Who are you, who rebuke me so sternly? Oh, you have shaken me to the depths of my terrified heart!

Paphnutius
I would that you could be shaken with fear to your very bowels! I would like to see your delicate body impregnated with terror in every vein, and every fibre, if that would keep you from yielding to the dangerous delights of the flesh.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
不如怜悯那些被你夺去得见上帝之福的灵魂——那正是你所承认的上帝！啊，泰绮丝，你存心冒犯了神圣的至尊。这便是你的定罪。

泰绮丝
你是什么意思？为何这样威吓我？

帕夫努提乌斯
因为你若留在罪中，地狱之火的刑罚正等着你。

泰绮丝
你是谁，这样严厉地责备我？啊，你把我惊恐的心底都撼动了！

帕夫努提乌斯
但愿恐惧能透入你的腑脏！我倒想看见你娇柔的身体每一条血脉、每一缕纤维都浸满恐怖，只要那能使你不致屈从于肉体那些危险的爱欲。

===Original===
Thais
And what zest for pleasure do you think is left now in a heart suddenly awakened to a consciousness of guilt! Remorse has killed everything.

Paphnutius
I long to see the thorns of vice cut away, and the choked-up fountain of your tears flowing once more. Tears of repentance are precious in the sight of God.

Thais
Oh, voice that promises mercy! Do you believe, can you hope that one so vile as I, soiled by thousands and thousands of impurities, can make reparation, can ever by any manner of penance obtain pardon?

Paphnutius
Thais, no sin is so great, no crime so black, that it cannot be expiated by tears and penitence, provided they are followed up by deeds.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
一颗蓦然醒悟到罪的心，你以为还剩得下多少享乐的兴致！悔恨已杀死了一切。

帕夫努提乌斯
我渴望看见罪恶的荆棘被铲除，你那壅塞的泪泉重新流淌。悔改的眼泪在上帝眼中是宝贵的。

泰绮丝
啊，这应许怜悯的声音！你相信吗，你能希望像我这样卑污的人、被千万重污秽玷染的人，能够补赎，能够借着任何苦修获得赦免吗？

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝，没有一种罪大到、没有一种恶黑到不能用眼泪与忏悔来抵偿——只要后面跟着相应的行动。

===Original===
Thais
Show me, I beg you, my father, what I can do to be reconciled with Him I have offended.

Paphnutius
Despise the world. Leave your dissolute lovers.

Thais
And afterwards? What then?

Paphnutius
You must retire to some solitary place, where you may learn to know yourself and realize the enormity of your sins.

Thais
If you think this will save me, I will not delay a moment.

Paphnutius
I have no doubt it will.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
求你指教我，我的神父，我当作什么，才能与我所冒犯的那一位和好。

帕夫努提乌斯
轻看世界。离开你那些放荡的情人。

泰绮丝
然后呢？再然后呢？

帕夫努提乌斯
你必须退隐到一处僻静之地，在那里学会认识自己，看清你的罪何等深重。

泰绮丝
你若认为这样能救我，我一刻也不耽搁。

帕夫努提乌斯
我毫不怀疑。

===Original===
Thais
Yet give me a little time. I must collect the wealth that I have gained through the sins of my body—all the treasures I have kept too long.

Paphnutius
Do not give them a moment's thought. There will be no lack of people to find them and make use of them.

Thais
I have another idea in my mind. I did not think of keeping this wealth or of giving it to my friends. Nor would I distribute it among the poor. The wages of sin are no material for good works.

Paphnutius
You are right. What then do you propose to do with your possessions?

Thais
Give them to the flames! Burn them to ashes!

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
不过给我一点时间。我必须收拾我用身体的罪换来的财物——那些我藏得太久的珍宝。

帕夫努提乌斯
一刻也不必惦念它们。不愁没人找到它们、利用它们。

泰绮丝
我心里另有主意。我不打算留下这些财物，也不打算送给我的朋友们，更不会分给穷人。罪的工价做不成善工的材料。

帕夫努提乌斯
你说得对。那你打算怎么处置你的财物？

泰绮丝
把它们交给烈火！烧成灰烬！

===Original===
Paphnutius
For what reason?

Thais
That they may no longer exist in the world. Each one was acquired at the cost of an injury to the goodness and beauty of the Creator. Let them burn.

Paphnutius
How you are changed! Grace is on your lips! Your eyes are calm, and impure passions no longer burn in them. Oh, miracle! Is this Thais who was once so greedy for gold? Is this Thais, who seeks so humbly the feet of God?

Thais
God give me grace to change still more. My heart is changed, but this mortal substance—how shall it be changed?

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
为什么？

泰绮丝
好让它们不再存在于世上。每一件都是伤害创造者的美善换来的。烧掉它们吧。

帕夫努提乌斯
你变得多么不同！恩典已在你唇上！你的眼睛安定了，淫邪的情欲不再在其中燃烧。啊，奇迹！这就是那个曾对黄金如此贪恋的泰绮丝吗？这就是如此谦卑地寻求上帝之足的泰绮丝吗？

泰绮丝
愿上帝赐我恩典，让我变得更多。我的心已改变，可这必朽的身体——它当如何改变？

===Original===
Paphnutius
It is not difficult for the unchangeable substance to transform us.

Thais
Now I am going to carry out my plan. Fire shall destroy everything I have.

Paphnutius
Go in peace. Then return to me here quickly. Do not delay! I trust your resolution, and yet—

Thais
You need not be afraid.

Paphnutius
Thais, come back quickly! God be with you!

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
对那不变的本体来说，改变我们并非难事。

泰绮丝
现在我就去实行我的计划。烈火将毁掉我的一切。

帕夫努提乌斯
平安地去吧。然后快快回到我这里。不要耽搁！我信你的决心，然而——

泰绮丝
你不必害怕。

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝，快回来！愿上帝与你同在！

#### Scene IV / 第四场

===Original===
Thais
Come, my lovers! Come, all my evil lovers! Hasten, my lovers! Your Thais calls you!

Lovers
That is the voice of Thais. She calls us. Let us make haste. Let us make haste, for by delay we may offend her.

Thais
Come, lovers! Run! Hasten! What makes you so slow? Never has Thais been more impatient for your coming. Come nearer. I have something to tell you all.

Lovers
Oh, Thais, what is the meaning of this pile of faggots? Why are you throwing all those beautiful and precious treasures on the pile?

Thais
You cannot guess? You do not know why I have built this fire?

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
来吧，我的情人们！来吧，我所有邪恶的情人们！快来，我的情人们！你们的泰绮丝在召唤你们！

情人们
那是泰绮丝的声音。她在召唤我们。快去。快去，迟了也许会触怒她。

泰绮丝
来吧，情人们！跑起来！快点！你们怎么这样慢？泰绮丝从来没有像现在这样急切盼你们到来。靠近些。我有话要告诉你们所有人。

情人们
啊，泰绮丝，这堆柴薪是什么意思？你为什么把那些美丽珍贵的珠宝都扔到柴堆上？

泰绮丝
你们猜不到？你们不知道我为什么生起这堆火？

===Original===
Lovers
We are amazed. We wonder greatly what is the meaning of it and of your strange looks.

Thais
You would like me to tell you, evil lovers?

Lovers
We long to hear.

Thais
Look, then!

Lovers
Stop, Thais! What are you doing? Are you mad?

===Chinese===
情人们
我们惊呆了。实在不明白这一切，还有你这奇怪的神色，究竟是什么意思。

泰绮丝
你们想让我告诉你们吗，邪恶的情人们？

情人们
我们亟欲一听。

泰绮丝
那就看吧！

情人们
住手，泰绮丝！你在干什么？你疯了吗？

===Original===
Thais
I am not mad. For the first time I am sane, and I rejoice!

Lovers
To waste these pounds of gold, and all the other treasure! Oh, Thais, you have lost your senses! These are beautiful things, precious things, and you burn them!

Thais
All these things I have extorted from you as the price of shameful deeds. I burn them to destroy all hope in you that I shall ever again turn to your love. And now I leave you.

Lovers
Wait, Thais. Oh wait a little, and tell us what has changed you!

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
我没有疯。我头一回清醒了，而且满心欢喜！

情人们
把这些磅重的黄金，连同其余的珍宝统统糟蹋掉！啊，泰绮丝，你失去理智了！这些都是美的东西、贵重的东西，你竟烧掉它们！

泰绮丝
所有这些东西，都是我从你们手里榨取的羞耻行径的价钱。我烧掉它们，是要断绝你们的一切指望——指望我再回转去爱你们。如今我离开你们。

情人们
等等，泰绮丝。啊，稍等片刻，告诉我们是什么改变了你！

===Original===
Thais
I will not stay. I will not tell you anything. To talk with you has become loathsome.

Lovers
What have we done to deserve this scorn and contempt? Can you accuse us of being unfaithful? What wrong have we done? We have always sought to satisfy your desires. And now you show us this bitter hatred! Unjust woman, what have we done?

Thais
Leave me, or let me leave you. Do not touch me. You can tear my garments, but you shall not touch me.

Lovers
Cruel Thais, speak to us! Before you go, speak to us!

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
我不留下。我什么也不会告诉你们。同你们说话已令我憎厌。

情人们
我们做了什么，该受这样的轻蔑与鄙弃？你能指控我们不忠吗？我们做错了什么？我们一直设法满足你的欲望。如今你却向我们显出这般刻骨的憎恨！不义的女人啊，我们究竟做了什么？

泰绮丝
放开我，不然就让我离开你们。不要碰我。你们可以撕碎我的衣裳，但休想碰我。

情人们
狠心的泰绮丝，对我们说句话吧！走之前，对我们说句话吧！

===Original===
Thais
I have sinned with you. But now is the end of sin, and all our wild pleasures are ended.

Lovers
Thais, do not leave us! Thais, where are you going?

Thais
Where none of you will ever see me again!

Lovers
What monstrous thing is this? Thais, glory of our land, is changed! Thais, our delight, who loved riches and power and luxury—Thais, who gave herself up to pleasure day and night, has destroyed past remedy gold and gems that had no price! What monstrous thing is this? Thais, the very flower of love, insults her lovers and scorns their gifts. Thais, whose boast it was that whoever loved her should enjoy her love! What monstrous thing is this? Thais! Thais! this is a thing not to be believed.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
我曾与你们一同犯罪。但如今罪到头了，我们一切放纵的欢愉都结束了。

情人们
泰绮丝，不要离开我们！泰绮丝，你要去哪里？

泰绮丝
去一个你们谁也再见不到我的地方！

情人们
这是何等骇人听闻的事？泰绮丝，我们土地的荣耀，变了！泰绮丝，我们的欢愉，那个爱财、爱势、爱奢侈的泰绮丝——那个昼夜沉溺于享乐的泰绮丝，竟把无价的黄金与宝石毁得无可挽回！这是何等骇人听闻的事？泰绮丝，爱的花朵，竟羞辱她的情人、鄙弃他们的馈赠！泰绮丝，她曾夸口说凡爱她者必得享受她的爱！这是何等骇人听闻的事？泰绮丝！泰绮丝！这真是叫人无法相信的事。

#### Scene V / 第五场

===Original===
Thais
Paphnutius, my father, I am ready now to obey you, command what you will.

Paphnutius
Thais, I have been uneasy during your absence. I feared you had been caught in the world's snare. I feared you would not return.

Thais
You need not have been afraid. The world does not tempt me now. My possessions are ashes. I have publicly renounced my lovers.

Paphnutius
Oh, happy guilt that has brought such happy penitence! Since you have renounced your earthly lovers, you can now be joined to your Heavenly Lover.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
帕夫努提乌斯，我的神父，如今我已预备好顺服你，随你吩咐。

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝，你不在的这段时间我心神不宁。我怕你落入世界的罗网，怕你一去不返。

泰绮丝
你本不必害怕。如今世界引不动我了。我的财物已成灰烬。我已当众弃绝了我的情人们。

帕夫努提乌斯
啊，这带来了何等幸福之悔改的、有福的罪！你既已弃绝地上的情人，如今便可许配给你的天上之爱了。

===Original===
Thais
It is for you to show me the way. Be a lantern to me, for all is obscure night.

Paphnutius
Trust me, daughter. Follow me.

Thais
I can follow you with my feet. Would that I could follow you with my deeds!

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
当由你为我引路。做我的灯笼吧，因为四下皆是晦暗的黑夜。

帕夫努提乌斯
信靠我，女儿。跟随我。

泰绮丝
我的脚能跟随你。但愿我的行为也能跟随你！

#### Scene VI / 第六场

===Original===
Thais
Oh, I am weary!

Paphnutius
Courage! Here is the monastery where a famous community of holy virgins live. I am anxious for you to pass the time of penance here if you will consent.

Thais
I do not resist. I wish to obey you. I trust you.

Paphnutius
I will go in, and persuade the Abbess who is the head of the community to receive you.

Thais
And what shall I do meanwhile? Do not leave me alone.

Paphnutius
You shall come with me. But look! The Abbess has come out to meet us. I wonder who can have told her so promptly of our arrival.

Thais
Rumour, Father Paphnutius. Rumour never delays.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
啊，我累了！

帕夫努提乌斯
振作！这里就是那座修道院，住着一群著名的圣洁童贞女。你若肯应允，我盼你在这里度过补赎的时日。

泰绮丝
我不抗拒。我愿顺服你。我信靠你。

帕夫努提乌斯
我进去劝说这位掌管修道团体的女院长收留你。

泰绮丝
那我在这期间做什么？不要留我独自一人。

帕夫努提乌斯
你随我同来。看！院长已出来迎我们了。不知是谁这样快就把我们到来的消息告诉了她。

泰绮丝
是流言，帕夫努提乌斯神父。流言从不耽搁。

#### Scene VII / 第七场

===Original===
Paphnutius
You come opportunely, illustrious Abbess. I was just seeking you.

Abbess
You are most welcome, venerated Father Paphnutius. Blessed is your visit, beloved of the Most High.

Paphnutius
May the grace of Him Who is Father of all pour into your heart the beatitude of everlasting peace!

Abbess
And what has brought your holiness to my humble dwelling?

Paphnutius
I need your help.

Abbess
Speak but the word. You will find me eager to do all in my power to carry out your wishes.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
您来得正好，尊贵的院长。我正要找您。

院长
竭诚欢迎，可敬的帕夫努提乌斯神父。蒙至高者所爱的您，此行有福了。

帕夫努提乌斯
愿那为万有之父的祂的恩典，将永福的平安倾入您心中！

院长
是何事使圣善的您光临我这寒陋的居所？

帕夫努提乌斯
我需要您的帮助。

院长
但说无妨。您将看到我竭尽全力成全您的意愿。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Oh, Abbess, I have brought you a little wild gazelle who has been snatched half dead from the jaws of wolves. Show it compassion, nurse it with all your tenderness, until it has shed its rough goatskin and put on the soft fleece of a lamb.

Abbess
Explain yourself further.

Paphnutius
You see this woman. From her youth she has led the life of a harlot. She has given herself up to base pleasures—

Abbess
What misery!

Paphnutius
She cannot offer the excuse that she was a Pagan to whom such pleasures bring no remorse of conscience. She wore the baptismal robes of a child of God when she gave herself to the flames of profane love. She was not tempted. She chose this evil life. She was ruined by her own will.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
啊，院长，我给您带来了一只小小的野瞪羚，它是从狼口中半死不活地夺回来的。请怜恤它，以您全部的温柔看护它，直到它褪去粗糙的山羊皮，披上羔羊柔软的绒毛。

院长
请再说得明白些。

帕夫努提乌斯
您看见这个女人了。她自青年时代起就过着妓女的生活，放纵于卑贱的欢愉——

院长
何等的不幸！

帕夫努提乌斯
她不能借口自己是异教徒，说这样的行径不会使异教徒的良心不安。她投身于淫乱爱欲的火焰时，身上穿的正是上帝儿女的洗礼之衣。她并非受了诱惑。是她自己选择了这邪恶的生活。她是毁于自己的意志。

===Original===
Abbess
She is the more unfortunate.

Paphnutius
Yet such is the power of Christ, that at His word, of which my poor mouth was the instrument, she has fled from the surroundings which were her damnation. Obedient as a child, she has followed me. She has abandoned lust and ease and idle luxury. She is resolved to live chastely.

Abbess
Glory to the Author of the marvellous change!

Paphnutius
Amen. But since the maladies of the soul, like those of the body, need physic for their cure, we must minister to this soul diseased by years of lust. It must be removed from the foul breath of the world. A narrow cell, solitude, silence—these must be her lot henceforth. She must learn to know herself and her sins.

Abbess
You are right. Such a penance is necessary.

===Chinese===
院长
那她愈发不幸了。

帕夫努提乌斯
然而基督的大能正是如此：借着祂的话语——我这卑微的口不过是器皿——她已逃离那使她沉沦的环境。她像孩子一样顺服，跟随我而来。她已弃绝了情欲、安逸与虚浮的奢华，决意贞洁地生活。

院长
愿荣耀归于这奇妙转变的创始者！

帕夫努提乌斯
阿们。但灵魂的疾病既如身体的疾病一样需要药物治疗，我们就必须医治这个被多年情欲侵蚀的病魂。它必须远离世界的浊气。一间窄小的斗室、孤独、静默——这些必须是她此后的分。她必须学会认识自己和自己的罪。

院长
您说得对。这样的补赎是必需的。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Will you give orders for a little cell to be made ready as soon as possible?

Abbess
Yes, my father. It shall be done as quickly as we can.

Paphnutius
There must be no entrance, no opening of any kind, except a small window through which she can receive the food that will be brought her on certain days at certain fixed hours. A pound of bread, and water according to her need.

Abbess
Forgive me, dear father in God, but I fear she will not be able to endure such a rigorous life. The soul may be willing, but that fastidious mind, that delicate body used to luxury, how can we expect them to submit?

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
您能吩咐人尽快备好一间小斗室吗？

院长
好的，神父。我们尽快办好。

帕夫努提乌斯
不得有门，不得有任何开口，只留一扇小窗，好在指定的日子、固定的时辰从那里递进她的食物。一磅面包，按她的需要供水。

院长
恕我直言，亲爱的上帝之仆，我怕她受不住这样严苛的生活。灵魂或许甘愿，可那娇惯的心思、那奢华惯了的娇柔身体，怎能指望它们屈服？

===Original===
Paphnutius
Have no fear. We know that grave sin demands a grave remedy.

Abbess
That is true, yet are we not told also to hasten slowly?

Paphnutius
Good mother, I am already weary of delay. What if her lovers should pursue her? What if she be drawn back into the abyss? I am impatient to see her enclosed.

Abbess
Nothing stands in the way of your enclosing her now. The cell which you told us to prepare is ready.

Paphnutius
Then enter, Thais! This is just such a refuge as we spoke of on our journey. It is the very place for you. There is room and more than room here for you to weep over your sins.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
不要怕。我们知道，重罪需要猛药。

院长
这话不错，可我们不也受教要「缓缓地急进」吗？

帕夫努提乌斯
好母亲，我已厌倦拖延。倘若她的情人们追来缠她怎么办？倘若她被拖回深渊怎么办？我急于见到她被幽禁起来。

院长
现在已没有任何事拦阻您幽禁她了。您吩咐我们预备的斗室已经妥当。

帕夫努提乌斯
那就进来吧，泰绮丝！这正是我们路上所说的那种避难之所。恰恰是为你而设的地方。这里尽有余地——绰绰有余——供你为你的罪哭泣。

===Original===
Thais
How small it is! How dark! How can a delicate woman live in such a place?

Paphnutius
You are not pleased with your new dwelling! You shudder at the thought of entering! Oh, Thais, have you not wandered long enough without restraint? Is it not right that you should now be confined in this narrow, solitary cell, where you will find true freedom?

Thais
I have been so long accustomed to pleasure and distraction. My mind is still a slave to the senses.

Paphnutius
The more need to rein it, to discipline it, until it ceases to rebel.

Thais
I do not rebel—but my weakness revolts against one thing here.

Paphnutius
Of what do you speak?

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
这么小！这么暗！一个娇弱的女人怎能在这样的地方生活？

帕夫努提乌斯
你不满意你的新居！一想到进去就发抖！啊，泰绮丝，你放任无拘地游荡得还不够久吗？如今你被禁闭在这窄小孤寂的斗室里——你将在这里找到真正的自由——这岂不正合适吗？

泰绮丝
我长久习惯了享乐与消遣。我的心仍是感官的奴仆。

帕夫努提乌斯
那就更需勒住它、管教它，直到它不再反叛。

泰绮丝
我不反叛——只是我的软弱在此抗拒一件事。

帕夫努提乌斯
你说的是什么？

===Original===
Thais
I am ashamed to say.

Paphnutius
Speak, Thais! Be ashamed of nothing but your sins.

Thais
Good father, what could be more repugnant than to have to attend to all the needs of the body in this one little room.   … It will soon be uninhabitable.

Paphnutius
Fear the cruel punishments of the soul, and cease to dread transitory evils.

Thais
My weakness makes me shudder.

Paphnutius
The sweetness of your guilty pleasures was far more bitter and foul.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
我羞于启齿。

帕夫努提乌斯
说吧，泰绮丝！除了你的罪，什么都不必羞愧。

泰绮丝
好神父，还有什么比不得不在这一间小屋里料理身体的一切需要更难堪的呢。……这屋子很快就没法住人了。

帕夫努提乌斯
当惧怕灵魂的严酷刑罚，不要再怕那转瞬即逝的苦楚。

泰绮丝
我的软弱使我战栗。

帕夫努提乌斯
你那些罪中之乐的甘甜，远比这更苦、更污秽。

===Original===
Thais
I know it is just. What grieves me most is that I shall not have one clean sweet spot in which to call upon the sweet name of God.

Paphnutius
Have a care, Thais, or your confidence may become presumption. Should polluted lips utter so easily the name of the unpolluted Godhead?

Thais
Oh, how can I hope for pardon! Who will pity me—who save me! What shall I do if I am forbidden to invoke Him against Whom only I have sinned! To whom should I pray if not to Him.

Paphnutius
You must indeed pray to Him, but with tears, not with words. Let not a tinkling voice, but the mighty roar of a contrite heart sound in the ear of God.

Thais
I desire His pardon. Surely I may ask for it?

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
我知道这是公义的。最让我悲伤的是，我竟没有一方洁净香甜的角落，可以在其中呼求上帝甘美的名。

帕夫努提乌斯
当心，泰绮丝，免得你的信赖变成僭妄。污秽的唇，岂可这样轻易地诵出那不受玷污的神性之名？

泰绮丝
啊，我怎能指望赦免！谁会怜悯我——谁救我！倘若不许我祈求那唯独被我所冒犯的祂，我可怎么办！不向祂祈祷，还能向谁祈祷呢。

帕夫努提乌斯
你固然要向祂祈祷，但要凭眼泪，不凭言语。愿在上帝耳中响起的，不是叮咚的嗓音，而是痛悔之心的雄壮咆哮。

泰绮丝
我渴望祂的赦免。我总可以求它吧？

===Original===
Paphnutius
Oh, Thais, the more perfectly you humble yourself, the more swiftly you will win it! Let your heart be all prayer, but let your lips say only this: "O God Who made me, pity me!"

Thais
O God, Who made me, pity me! He alone can save me from defeat in this hard struggle!

Paphnutius
Fight manfully, and you will gain a glorious victory.

Thais
It is your part to pray for me! Pray I may earn the victor's palm.

Paphnutius
You need not remind me.

Thais
Give me some hope!

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
啊，泰绮丝，你越是完全地谦卑自己，便越快赢得它！让你的心全然成为祈祷，让你的唇只说这一句：「造我的上帝啊，怜悯我！」

泰绮丝
造我的上帝啊，怜悯我！唯独祂能救我不败于这场艰难的争战！

帕夫努提乌斯
像男子汉一样战斗，你必赢得光荣的胜利。

泰绮丝
为我祈祷乃是你的本分！祈祷我能赢得得胜者的棕榈枝。

帕夫努提乌斯
不必你来提醒。

泰绮丝
给我一点盼望吧！

===Original===
Paphnutius
Courage! The palm will soon be in this humble hand. It is time for me to return to the desert. I owe a duty to my dear disciples. I know their hearts are torn by my absence. Yes. I must go. Venerable Abbess, I trust this captive to our charity and tenderness. I beg you to take the best care of her. Sustain her delicate body with necessaries. Refresh her soul with the luxuries of divine knowledge.

Abbess
Have no anxiety about her, for I will cherish her with a mother's love and tenderness.

Paphnutius
I go then.

Abbess
In peace.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
振作！棕榈枝很快就要握在这只谦卑的手中了。我该回沙漠去了。我对我亲爱的弟子们负有本分。我知道他们的心正为我的缺席而撕裂。是的。我必须走了。可敬的院长，我将这位被掳者托付给我们的慈善与温柔。恳请您妥善照料她。以必需之物维持她娇弱的身体，以神圣知识的珍馐滋养她的灵魂。

院长
不必为她挂虑，我必以母亲的爱与温柔珍爱她。

帕夫努提乌斯
那我走了。

院长
平安地去吧。

#### Scene VIII / 第八场

===Original===
Disciples
Who knocks there?

Paphnutius
It is I—your father.

Disciples
It is the voice of our father Paphnutius!

Paphnutius
Unbolt the door.

Disciple
Good father, welcome.

All
Welcome, father! Welcome!

Paphnutius
A blessing on you all!

===Chinese===
众弟子
谁在敲门？

帕夫努提乌斯
是我——你们的神父。

众弟子
这是我们帕夫努提乌斯神父的声音！

帕夫努提乌斯
拔开门闩。

弟子
好神父，欢迎您。

众人
欢迎，神父！欢迎！

帕夫努提乌斯
愿祝福降临你们众人！

===Original===
Disciple
You have given us great uneasiness by your long absence.

Paphnutius
It has been fruitful.

Disciple
Your mission has succeeded? Come, tell us what has happened to Thais.

Paphnutius
All that I wished.

Disciple
She has abandoned her evil life?

Paphnutius
Yes.

===Chinese===
弟子
您离去这样久，让我们惶惶不安。

帕夫努提乌斯
此行结了果子。

弟子
您的使命成功了？来，告诉我们泰绮丝怎样了。

帕夫努提乌斯
一切如我所愿。

弟子
她弃绝了那种恶浊的生活？

帕夫努提乌斯
是的。

===Original===
Disciple
Where is she living now?

Paphnutius
She weeps over her sins in a little cell.

Disciples
Praise be to the Supreme Trinity!

Paphnutius
A little narrow cell, no wider than a grave. Blessed be His Terrible Name now and forever.

Disciples
Amen.

===Chinese===
弟子
她如今住在哪里？

帕夫努提乌斯
她在一间小斗室里为她的罪哭泣。

众弟子
愿赞美归于至高的三位一体！

帕夫努提乌斯
一间窄小的斗室，不比一座坟墓更宽。愿祂可畏的名，从今时直到永远，蒙受赞美。

众弟子
阿们。

#### Scene IX / 第九场

===Original===
Paphnutius
Three years of her penance are over, and I cannot tell whether her sorrow has found favour with God. For some reason He will not enlighten me. I know what I will do. I will go to my brother Antony and beg him to intercede for me. God will make the truth known to him.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
她的补赎已满三年，我却无从知晓她的忧伤是否蒙上帝悦纳。出于某种缘故，祂不愿光照我。我知道我当作什么。我要去找我的弟兄安东尼，求他为我代求。上帝必向他显明真相。

#### Scene X / 第十场

===Original===
Antony
Who comes this way? By his dress it is some brother-dweller in the desert. My old eyes do not recognize you yet, friend. Come nearer.

Paphnutius
Brother Antony! Do you not know me?

Antony
This is joy indeed! What pleasures God sends us, when we resign ourselves to have none! I did not think to see my brother Paphnutius again in this world. Is it indeed you, brother?

Paphnutius
Yes, it is I.

Antony
You are welcome, very welcome. Your coming gives me great joy.

Paphnutius
I am no less rejoiced to see you.

===Chinese===
安东尼
谁往这边来了？看这身装束，是位沙漠中的隐修弟兄。我这双老眼一时认不出你，朋友。走近些。

帕夫努提乌斯
安东尼弟兄！您认不出我了吗？

安东尼
这真是喜乐！当我们甘心舍弃欢愉时，上帝竟赐下这样的乐事！我原以为此生再见不到我的弟兄帕夫努提乌斯了。真的是你吗，弟兄？

帕夫努提乌斯
是，是我。

安东尼
欢迎你，万分欢迎。你的到来给我莫大的喜乐。

帕夫努提乌斯
见到您，我同样欢喜。

===Original===
Antony
But what is the cause? What has brought Paphnutius from his solitary retreat? He is not sick, I trust? He has not come to old Antony for healing?

Paphnutius
No, I am in good health.

Antony
That's well! I am glad of it.

Paphnutius
Brother Antony, it is three years since my peace was broken and disturbed by the persistent vision of a soul in peril. I heard a voice calling me night and day. But I stopped my ears—fearing my weakness. I thought "She calls me to ruin me." "No, no," the voice said. "I call you to save me."

Antony
A woman's voice!

===Chinese===
安东尼
可究竟为何？是什么把帕夫努提乌斯从他幽居之处引出来了？他没生病吧？总不是来找老安东尼求医治的吧？

帕夫努提乌斯
没有，我身子康健。

安东尼
那就好！我很欣慰。

帕夫努提乌斯
安东尼弟兄，三年来，一个身处险境之灵魂的异象纠缠不休，打破并搅扰了我的安宁。我听见一个声音昼夜呼唤我。可我捂住了耳朵——惧怕自己的软弱。我想：「她唤我，是要毁我。」「不，不是，」那声音说。「我唤你，是要你救我。」

安东尼
一个女人的声音！

===Original===
Paphnutius
Before my vision it was well known to us all that in the great town on the edge of the desert there was a harlot called Thais, through whom many were destroyed body and soul.

Antony
It was she who called you!

Paphnutius
Brother Antony, it was God who called me. My disciples opposed me; nevertheless I went to the town to see Thais and wrestle with the demon.

Antony
A perilous enterprise.

Paphnutius
I went to her in the disguise of a lover, and began by flattering her with sweet words. Then I threw off the mask and brought terror to her soul with bitter reproaches and threats of God's punishment.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
在我见异象之前，我们大家早都知道，沙漠边缘的大城里有一个名叫泰绮丝的妓女，许多人因她而身魂俱毁。

安东尼
是她在呼唤你！

帕夫努提乌斯
安东尼弟兄，是上帝在呼唤我。我的弟子们曾拦阻我；我仍去了那城，去见泰绮丝，与魔鬼角力。

安东尼
一桩凶险的事业。

帕夫努提乌斯
我乔装成情人去见她，先用甜言蜜语奉承她。随后我揭去面具，以痛切的责备和上帝惩罚的威吓，将恐怖带进她的灵魂。

===Original===
Antony
A prudent course. Hard words are necessary when natures have grown soft and can no longer distinguish between good and evil.

Paphnutius
I was disarmed by her docility. Truly, brother Antony, my heart melted like wax when she spurned her ill-gotten wealth and abandoned her lovers.

Antony
But you hid your tenderness?

Paphnutius
Yes, Brother Antony.

Antony
What followed?

Paphnutius
She chose to live in chastity. She consented to be enclosed in a narrow cell. She accepted her penance with sweetness and humility.

===Chinese===
安东尼
审慎的做法。当天性已变得疲软、再不能分辨善恶时，严厉的言语是必需的。

帕夫努提乌斯
她的温顺解除了我的武装。说真的，安东尼弟兄，当她唾弃不义之财、抛弃情人们时，我的心如蜡熔化。

安东尼
可你藏起了你的柔情？

帕夫努提乌斯
是的，安东尼弟兄。

安东尼
后来呢？

帕夫努提乌斯
她选择贞洁地生活。她同意被幽禁于一间窄小的斗室。她以甘美与谦卑接受了她的补赎。

===Original===
Antony
I am rejoiced by what you have told me! All the blood in my old veins exults and rejoices!

Paphnutius
That is because you are a saint.

Antony
Brother, you cannot mean that you are sad?

Paphnutius
I rejoice immeasurably in her conversion. Yet at times I am uneasy. I fear that the penance may have been too long and severe for a woman of such delicate frame.

Antony
That does you no wrong. Where true love is, loving compassion is not wanting.

===Chinese===
安东尼
你所说的话使我喜乐！我老迈血管里的每一滴血都在欢腾雀跃！

帕夫努提乌斯
那是因为您是圣者。

安东尼
弟兄，你不会是说你自己忧伤吧？

帕夫努提乌斯
她的皈依使我欢欣无量。然而我时常不安。我怕这补赎对一个身躯如此娇弱的女子来说，未免太长、太严酷了。

安东尼
这于你无损。哪里有真爱，哪里就不缺仁慈的怜恤。

===Original===
Paphnutius
I came to beg yours for Thais. Of your charity give me your prayers. I beg you and your disciples to join with me in praying for a sign. Let us persevere in prayer until it is shown us from heaven that the penitent's tears have moved the divine mercy to indulgence.

Antony
Brother Paphnutius, I have never granted a request more gladly. Come, we will gather together my disciples.

Paphnutius
I know that God will listen to his good servant Antony.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
我就是来为泰绮丝求这怜恤的。请大发慈善，把您的祷告给我。恳求您与您的弟子们同我一起祈求一个征兆。让我们恒切祈祷，直到上天向我们显明：悔罪者的眼泪已感动神圣的怜悯施以宽宥。

安东尼
帕夫努提乌斯弟兄，我还从未如此欣然应允过一个请求。来，我们去把我的弟子们聚齐。

帕夫努提乌斯
我知道上帝必垂听祂的良仆安东尼。

#### Scene XI / 第十一场

===Original===
Antony
Thanks be to God! The gospel's promise is fulfilled in us!

Paphnutius
What promise, blessed Antony?

Antony
Those who unite in prayer can obtain whatever they desire.

Paphnutius
What miracle has happened? What is it?

Antony
My disciple Paul has had a vision.

===Chinese===
安东尼
感谢上帝！福音的应许在我们身上应验了！

帕夫努提乌斯
什么应许，蒙福的安东尼？

安东尼
同心合意祈祷的人，凡他所愿的都能得着。

帕夫努提乌斯
发生了什么奇迹？是什么？

安东尼
我的弟子保罗得了一个异象。

===Original===
Paphnutius
What vision? Oh, call him!

Antony
He is here. Paul, my son, tell our brother, Paphnutius, the wonders you have seen.

Paul
Father, I saw in my vision a splendid bed. It was adorned with white hangings and coverings, and a crown was laid on it, and round it were four radiant virgins. They stood there as if they were guarding the crown. There was a great brightness round the bed, and a multitude of angels. I, seeing this wonderful and joyful sight, cried out, "This glory must be for my master and father Antony!"

Antony
Son, did you not know Antony was unworthy of such honour?

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
什么异象？啊，叫他来！

安东尼
他就在这里。保罗，我的孩子，把你所见的奇事讲给我们的弟兄帕夫努提乌斯听。

保罗
神父，我在异象中看见一张华美的床。床上缀着白色的帷幔与被衾，一顶冠冕安放在上面，床的四周围着四位发光的童贞女。她们站在那里，仿佛守护着那冠冕。床的四周大光环绕，还有天使的行列。我看见这奇妙喜乐的景象，不禁喊道：「这荣耀必是为我师父、神父安东尼预备的！」

安东尼
孩子，你不知道安东尼不配得这样的尊荣吗？

===Original===
Paul
But a divine voice answered me, saying, "This glory is prepared, not, as you think, for Antony, but for the harlot, Thais!"

Paphnutius
O sweet Christ! How shall I praise Thee for so lovingly sending comfort to my sad heart?

Antony
He is worthy to be praised.

Paphnutius
Then farewell, Brother Antony. I must go at once to my captive.

Antony
You must indeed. It is time her valiant penance ended. You should assure her that her pardon is complete; you should fill her with hope, and speak to her only of the beatitude in store for her.

Paphnutius
Your blessing.

===Chinese===
保罗
却有一个神圣的声音回答我说：「这荣耀不是为你所想的安东尼预备的，而是为那妓女泰绮丝预备的！」

帕夫努提乌斯
甘甜的基督啊！您如此慈爱地安慰我这忧伤的心，我当怎样赞美您？

安东尼
祂配得赞美。

帕夫努提乌斯
那就别过了，安东尼弟兄。我必须即刻回到我被掳的人那里去。

安东尼
的确该去了。她英勇的补赎是该结束的时候了。你当向她保证她的赦免已经完全；你当使她满怀盼望，只对她讲论那为她存留的福乐。

帕夫努提乌斯
请赐下您的祝福。

#### Scene XII / 第十二场

===Original===
Paphnutius
Thais, my little daughter! Thais! Open the window and let me see you.

Thais
Who speaks?

Paphnutius
Paphnutius.

Thais
Why should you visit a poor sinner? Why should I be given this great joy and happiness?

Paphnutius
These years that I have been absent from you in the body have been weary to me too. I have thought of you night and day. I have yearned for your salvation.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝，我的小女儿！泰绮丝！打开窗，让我看看你。

泰绮丝
谁在说话？

帕夫努提乌斯
帕夫努提乌斯。

泰绮丝
您为何来看望一个可怜的罪人？我为何被赐予这莫大的喜乐与幸福？

帕夫努提乌斯
这些年在身体上与你分离的岁月，对我也同样煎熬。我昼夜思念你，渴慕你的得救。

===Original===
Thais
I never doubted that.

Paphnutius
Tell me how things are with you. How have you lived here? What have you been doing?

Thais
Nothing worth the telling! I have nothing to offer God.

Paphnutius
The offering He loves best is a humble spirit.

Thais
All I have done is to gather up the many sins on my conscience into a mighty bundle and keep them always in mind. All day I have sat gazing towards the East, saying only this one prayer: "O God Who made me, pity me!" If my bodily senses have always been conscious of the offensiveness of this place, my heart's eyes have never been blind to the dreadfulness of hell.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
这我从不怀疑。

帕夫努提乌斯
告诉我你的近况。你在这里怎样生活？都做了些什么？

泰绮丝
没有什么值得一说的！我没有可献给上帝的。

帕夫努提乌斯
祂最爱的祭品，是痛悔谦卑的灵。

泰绮丝
我所做的，不过是把良心上堆积的众罪捆成一大捆，时刻放在心上。我整日坐着凝望东方，只说这一句祈祷：「造我的上帝啊，怜悯我！」我身体的感官固然时时感到此地的污秽难堪，而我心里的眼睛，也从未对地狱的可怖视而不见。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Your great penitence has won a great forgiveness. Yet God has not pardoned you for your valiant expiation so much as for the love with which you have given yourself to Christ.

Thais
Can that be true? Would that it were!

Paphnutius
Give me your hand. Let me bring you out of your cell to prove you are forgiven.

Thais
No, father, leave me here. This place with all its uncleanness is best for me.

Paphnutius
The time has come for you to cast away your fear, and hope for life! God wishes your penance to end.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
你深切的悔改赢得了丰盛的赦免。然而上帝赦免你，与其说是因你英勇的补赎，不如说是因你献身于基督的那份爱。

泰绮丝
这是真的吗？但愿如此！

帕夫努提乌斯
把手给我。让我把你带出斗室，证明你已蒙赦免。

泰绮丝
不，神父，留我在这里吧。这地方连同它的一切污秽，于我正合适。

帕夫努提乌斯
你抛却恐惧、盼望生命的时刻到了！上帝愿你的补赎结束。

===Original===
Thais
Let the angels praise Him! He has not despised the love of a humble sinner.

Paphnutius
Thais, would you rejoice if now you were called upon to lay aside this body?

Thais
Oh, father, my soul longs to escape from this earth.

Paphnutius
Thais, you have finished your course here. In fifteen days you will, by God's grace, pass straight to Paradise.

Thais
To Paradise! I should be happy if I might be spared hell's torments and be mercifully cleansed in a gentle fire until my spirit is fit for the eternal happiness.

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
愿众天使赞美祂！祂没有轻看一个谦卑罪人的爱。

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝，倘若此刻召你放下这身体，你会欢喜吗？

泰绮丝
啊，神父，我的灵魂渴望逃离这尘世。

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝，你在此世的行程已经走完。再过十五天，你将靠着上帝的恩典，径直进入乐园。

泰绮丝
进乐园！倘若我能免于地狱的酷刑，在温柔的火中蒙怜悯炼净，直到我的灵配得上那永恒的福乐，我就有福了。

===Original===
Paphnutius
Grace is the free gift of God and does not depend on our merits. If it did, it could not be called grace.

Thais
For this let the choirs of heaven praise Him, and all the little twigs and fresh green leaves on earth, all animals, and the great waters. He is patient with us when we fall! He is generous in His gifts when we repent.

Paphnutius
He loves to be merciful. From all eternity He has preferred pardon to punishment.

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
恩典是上帝白白的赏赐，并不依赖我们的功德。若依赖功德，它就不配称为恩典了。

泰绮丝
为此，愿天上的歌队赞美祂，愿地上一切细枝与鲜绿的叶子、一切走兽与大水都赞美祂。我们跌倒时，祂恒久忍耐！我们悔改时，祂慷慨施恩！

帕夫努提乌斯
祂乐于施怜悯。从亘古到永远，祂宁赦免而不惩罚。

#### Scene XIII / 第十三场

===Original===
Thais
Holy father, do not leave me. Be near to comfort me in this hour of my death.

Paphnutius
I will not leave you, Thais, until your soul has taken flight to the stars, and I have buried your body.

Thais
I feel the end is near. Brother, do not leave me!

===Chinese===
泰绮丝
圣善的神父，不要离开我。在我死亡的时刻，守在近旁安慰我。

帕夫努提乌斯
我不会离开你，泰绮丝，直到你的灵魂飞升星辰，直到我亲手葬了你的身体。

泰绮丝
我觉出终点近了。弟兄，不要离开我！

===Original===
Paphnutius
Now is the time to pray.

Thais
O God Who made me, pity me! Grant that the soul which Thou didst breathe into me may now happily return to Thee. O God Who made me, pity me!

Paphnutius
Thais! Thais! Oh, loving humble spirit, pass to thy glory!  … Angels lead her into Paradise!  … O uncreated Beauty, existing in Truth without material form, grant that the divers parts of this human body now to be dissolved may return to their original elements! Grant that the soul, given from on high, may soar into light and joy, and that the body may be cherished peacefully in the soft lap of the earth until that day when, the ashes being brought together again, and the life-giving sap restored to the veins, this same Thais may rise again, a perfect human being as before, and take her place among the glorious white flock who shall be led into the joy of eternity! Grant this, O Thou Who alone art what Thou art—Who livest and reignest and art glorious in the Unity and perfect Trinity through infinite ages!

===Chinese===
帕夫努提乌斯
现在是祈祷的时候了。

泰绮丝
造我的上帝啊，怜悯我！求您允准您吹入我里面的灵魂，如今欢喜地归回您那里。造我的上帝啊，怜悯我！

帕夫努提乌斯
泰绮丝！泰绮丝！啊，充满爱与谦卑的灵魂，去承受你的荣耀吧！……众天使引领她进入乐园！……啊，不受造之美，无形无相而存在于真理之中，求您允准这即将消解的人身各部归于它们原初的元素！求您允准那自上赐下的灵魂翱翔于光明与喜乐，允准这身体在大地柔软的怀中得享安息，直到那一日——灰烬重聚，生气的汁液重回血脉——这同一个泰绮丝复活过来，仍如从前一样成为完全的人，站在那光荣的白色羊群之中，被引入永恒的喜乐！求您应允，啊，您这惟一是您所是的那一位——您活着、掌权，在三一完美的合一中得享荣耀，直到无穷无尽的时代！

## Prefaces of Roswitha / 罗斯维塔的序言

### Preface to the Plays of Hrotswitha, German Religious and Virgin of the Saxon Race / 德意志修女、萨克森族贞女罗斯维塔剧作序

===Original===
There are many Catholics, and we cannot entirely acquit ourselves of the charge, who, attracted by the polished elegance of the style of pagan writers, prefer their works to the holy scriptures. There are others who, although they are deeply attached to the sacred writings and have no liking for most pagan productions, make an exception in favour of the works of Terence, and, fascinated by the charm of the manner, risk being corrupted by the wickedness of the matter. Wherefore I, the strong voice of Gandersheim, have not hesitated to imitate in my writings a poet whose works are so widely read, my object being to glorify, within the limits of my poor talent, the laudable chastity of Christian virgins in that selfsame form of composition which has been used to describe the shameless acts of licentious women. One thing has all the same embarrassed me and often brought a blush to my cheek. It is that I have been compelled through the nature of this work to apply my mind and my pen to depicting the dreadful frenzy of those possessed by unlawful love, and the insidious sweetness of passion﻿—things which should not even be named among us. Yet if from modesty I had refrained from treating these subjects I should not have been able to attain my object﻿—to glorify the innocent to the best of my ability. For the more seductive the blandishments of lovers the more wonderful the divine succour and the greater the merit of those who resist, especially when it is fragile woman who is victorious and strong man who is routed with confusion.

I have no doubt that many will say that my poor work is much inferior to that of the author whom I have taken as my model, that it is on a much humbler scale, and indeed altogether different.

===Chinese===
天主教徒中有许多人，为异教作家笔下典雅工丽的文风所吸引，宁取其作品而舍圣经——对此指摘，我们自己也难辞其咎。另有一些人，虽深爱圣典，对大多数异教著述并无好感，却对泰伦提乌斯的作品网开一面；他们为其笔致之妩媚所迷，便有被题材之邪恶所腐蚀的危险。因此之故，我，甘德斯海姆的强音，才毫不犹豫地在写作中模仿一位作品流传如此之广的诗人；我的目标是：在我菲薄才力所及的范围内，用那曾用来描写淫荡女子无耻行径的同一种文体，来颂扬基督徒贞女们值得赞美的贞洁。尽管如此，有一件事始终令我窘迫，常使我双颊绯红。那就是：受这部作品性质所迫，我不得不运思动笔，去描绘那些被非法爱欲所附身者的可怖癫狂，以及情欲的阴柔甘甜——这些事在我们中间连提都不该提。然而，倘若我出于羞怯而回避这些题材，我便无法达成我的目标——竭尽所能地颂扬无辜者。因为求爱者的蛊惑越是诱人，天主的援救便越是奇妙，抗拒者的功德便越是伟大——尤其当得胜者是柔弱的女子、溃逃者是刚强的男子之时。

我毫不怀疑，许多人会说，我这部拙作远逊于我所奉为楷模的那位作家，其格局卑微得多，实则全然是另一回事。

===Original===
Well, I do not deny this. None can justly accuse me of wishing to place myself on a level with those who by the sublimity of their genius have so far outstripped me. No, I am not so arrogant as to compare myself even with the least among the scholars of the ancient world. I strive only, although my power is not equal to my desire, to use what talent I have for the glory of Him Who gave it me. Nor is my self-love so great that I would, to avoid criticism, abstain from proclaiming wherever possible the virtue of Christ working in His saints. If this pious devotion gives satisfaction I shall rejoice; if it does not, either on account of my own worthlessness or of the faults of my unpolished style, I shall still be glad that I made the effort.

In the humbler works of my salad days I gathered up my poor researches in heroic strophes, but here I have sifted them into a series of dramatic scenes and avoided through omission the pernicious voluptuousness of pagan writers.

===Chinese===
好吧，对此我并不否认。谁也不能公允地指斥我，说我想将自己与那些凭天才之崇高而远远超越我的人相提并论。不，我还不至于傲慢到将自己与古代学者中最微末者相比。我只是勉力而为——尽管心力不逮夙愿——将仅有的才赋用于荣耀那赐我才赋的主。我的自爱也没有大到那种地步：为了免遭非议，便不去处处传扬基督在其圣徒身上运行的德能。倘若这份虔诚的奉献能令人满意，我自当欢喜；倘若不能——无论是因为我自身的无价值，还是因为我未加润饰的文风的缺陷——我仍将为自己做过这份努力而欣然。

在青涩年华那些较为卑微的作品里，我曾以史诗诗节收辑我可怜的研习所得；而在此处，我将它们筛入一连串戏剧场面之中，并借助省略，避开了异教作家那有害的淫艳之笔。

### Epistle of the Same to the Learned Patrons of This Book / 同一作者致本书博学恩主的书信

===Original===
To you, learned and virtuous men, who do not envy the success of others, but on the contrary rejoice in it as becomes the truly great, Hrotswitha, poor humble sinner, sends wishes for your health in this life and your joy in eternity.

===Chinese===
致你们这些博学而高尚的人——你们不妒忌他人的成功，反而如同真正伟大者所当为的那样为之欢喜——罗斯维塔，这贫穷卑微的罪人，谨祝诸君今生安康，并于永恒中得享喜乐。

===Original===
I cannot praise you enough for your humility, or pay an adequate tribute to your kindness and affection. To think that you, who have been nurtured in the most profound philosophical studies and have attained knowledge in perfection, should have deigned to approve the humble work of an obscure woman! You have, however, not praised me but the Giver of the grace which works in me, by sending me your paternal congratulations and admitting that I possess some little knowledge of those arts the subtleties of which exceed the grasp of my woman’s mind. Until I showed my work to you I had not dared to let anyone see it except my intimate companions. I came near abandoning this form of writing altogether, for if there were few to whom I could submit my compositions at all there were fewer still who could point out what needed correction and encourage me to go on. But now, reassured by your verdict (is it not said that the testimony of three witnesses is “equivalent to the truth”?), I feel that I have enough confidence to apply myself to writing, if God grants me the power, and that I need not fear the criticism of the learned whoever they may be. Still, I am torn by conflicting feelings. I rejoice from the depths of my soul that the God through Whose grace alone I am what I am should be praised in me, but I am afraid of being thought greater than I am. I know that it is as wrong to deny a divine gift as to pretend falsely that we have received it. So I will not deny that through the grace of the Creator I have acquired some knowledge of the arts. He has given me the ability to learn﻿—I am a teachable creature﻿—yet of myself I should know nothing. He has given me a perspicacious mind, but one that lies fallow and idle when it is not cultivated. That my natural gifts might not be made void by negligence I have been at pains, whenever I have been able to pick up some threads and scraps torn from the old mantle of philosophy, to weave them into the stuff of my own book, in the hope that my lowly ignorant effort may gain more acceptance through the introduction of something of a nobler strain, and that the Creator of genius may be the more honoured since it is generally believed that a woman’s intelligence is slower. Such has been my motive in writing, the sole reason for the sweat and fatigue which my labours have cost me. At least I do not pretend to have knowledge where I am ignorant. On the contrary, my best claim to indulgence is that I know how much I do not know.

Impelled by your kindly interest and your express wish I come, bowing low like a reed, to submit this little work to your judgment. I wrote it indeed with that idea in my mind, although doubt as to its merits has made me withhold it until now. I hope you will revise it with the same careful attention that you would give to a work of your own, and that when you have succeeded in bringing it up to the proper standard you will return it to me, that I may learn what are its worst faults.

===Chinese===
诸君的谦抑，我赞誉不尽；诸君的仁厚与慈爱，我更无以为报。你们自最精深的哲学研究中受教养，学识臻于至境，竟肯垂青一个无名女子的卑微之作，思之何胜感荷！然而，诸君所称赞的并非我，而是那在我身上运行之恩宠的赐予者：你们赐我慈父般的嘉勉，承认我对那些技艺略有所知——尽管其精微之处本非我这女子的心智所能尽窥。在将作品呈送诸君之前，除几位亲密的同伴外，我不敢让任何人过目。我一度几乎要彻底放弃这种写作；因为肯于接纳我文稿的人本已寥寥，能指出何处须加订正、并勉励我继续写下去的人，更是少之又少。而如今，得了诸君的裁断（岂不闻三人之见证即「等同于真实」么？），我便安心了：倘若天主赐我力量，我自信可以专志写作，且无论博学者为谁人，都不必畏惧他们的批评。然而，我心中仍被相互冲突的情感撕扯着。我自灵魂深处欢喜，因为那唯凭其恩宠我才得成为我的天主，在我身上受了颂赞；可我又惧怕被人估量得高于我的实际。我知道，妄称已受天赐与谎称未受天赐，其罪相当——否认一项神性恩赐之错，正如谎称自己已领受此项恩赐之错。所以我不否认：托造物主之恩，我确曾习得一些技艺知识。祂赐我可教之性——我本是可教的受造物——但凭我自己，本一无所知。祂赐我敏锐的心智，但这心智不经耕耘，便只能休耕闲置。为了不让疏懒废掉我的天资，我一有机会拾得几缕从哲学古袍上撕下的线头与碎布，便不辞辛苦地将它们织入我自己这本书的质料之中；我盼着我这卑微无知的努力，或因掺入几分更高贵的质素而多得几分接纳；也盼着天才的造物主因之更受尊荣——既然世人普遍认为，女子的智力较为迟缓。这便是我写作的动机，也是我的劳作使我流汗受累的唯一缘由。至少，在我不通之处，我不假装通晓。恰恰相反，我请求宽宥的最有力理由，正是我知道自己何其无知。

为诸君的仁厚关切与明言愿望所促，我如芦苇般深深俯首，将这小小的作品呈交诸君裁夺。我写作时心中确实怀着此念，只是对其价值的疑虑，使我一直扣留至今。我冀望诸君以对待自己著作的那份细心来审订它；待诸君将它修润到应有的水准之后，再将它归还于我，好让我得知它最严重的缺陷何在。

## Sapientia / 萨皮恩提亚

### Argument / 剧情

===Original===
The martyrdom of the holy virgins Faith, Hope, and Charity, who are put to the torture by the Emperor Hadrian and slain in the presence of their mother Sapientia, she encouraging them by her admonitions to bear their sufferings. After their death the holy mother recovers the bodies of her children, embalms them with spices, and buries them with honour about five miles outside the city of Rome.

Forty days later the spirit of Sapientia takes its flight to heaven while she is still praying by her children’s graves.

===Chinese===
圣处女信德（Faith）、望德（Hope）与爱德（Charity）的殉道：哈德良皇帝（Hadrian）对她们施以酷刑，并在她们的母亲萨皮恩提亚（Sapientia）面前将她们处死；母亲则以劝勉激励女儿们承受苦难。三女死后，这位圣洁的母亲收回孩子们的遗体，以香料涂敷，隆重安葬于罗马城外约五英里处。

四十天后，萨皮恩提亚仍在孩子们的墓旁祈祷之时，魂灵飞升天国。

### Characters / 人物

===Original===
- Antiochus
- Hadrian
- Sapientia
- Faith
- Hope
- Charity
- Matrons

===Chinese===
- 安条克
- 哈德良
- 萨皮恩提亚
- 信德
- 望德
- 爱德
- 贵妇们

### Sapientia / 萨皮恩提亚

#### Scene I / 第一场

===Original===
Antiochus
My Lord Emperor, what desire has your servant but to see you powerful and prosperous? What ambition apart from the welfare and peace and greatness of the state you rule? So when I discover anything that threatens the commonwealth or your peace of mind I try to crush it before it has taken root.

Hadrian
In this you show discretion, Antiochus. Our prosperity means your advantage. Witness the honours that we never tire of heaping on you.

Antiochus
Your Grace’s welfare is so dear to me that I do not seek to disguise what is hostile to your interests, but immediately bring it to your notice and denounce it!

Hadrian
Do you praise yourself for this? If you withheld such information you would be guilty of treason to our Imperial Majesty.

Antiochus
I have never been disloyal.

Hadrian
I do not question it. Come, if you have discovered some new danger, make it known to us.

===Chinese===
安条克（Antiochus）
陛下，仆臣别无所愿，唯愿见您权隆运盛。除了您所治国度的福祉、安宁与强盛之外，臣更复何求？因此每当发现有危害邦国、搅扰圣心安宁之事，臣必设法在它生根之前将其扑灭。

哈德良（Hadrian）
安条克，于此可见你的审慎。我们的昌盛即是你的利益。请看我们加于你的荣宠，从不厌倦。

安条克
陛下的安康于臣如此珍贵，凡有不利于陛下之事，臣决不遮掩，必即刻奏闻于您，加以告发！

哈德良
你为此自诩吗？你若隐匿此等消息不发，便构成对我皇威的叛罪。

安条克
臣从未怀有二心。

哈德良
朕并不置疑。说吧，你若发现了什么新的危险，便奏报于朕。

===Original===
Antiochus
A certain alien woman has recently come to this city with her three children.

Hadrian
Of what sex are the children?

Antiochus
They are all girls.

Hadrian
And you think that a handful of women threaten danger to the state?

Antiochus
I do, and very grave danger.

Hadrian
Of what kind?

===Chinese===
安条克
近日有一外邦女子，携其三个孩子来到本城。

哈德良
孩子是男是女？

安条克
皆为女孩。

哈德良
你以为区区几个女子就能危及邦国？

安条克
正是，而且是极严重的危险。

哈德良
何种危险？

===Original===
Antiochus
A disturbance of the peace.

Hadrian
How?

Antiochus
What disturbs the peace and harmony of states more than religious differences?

Hadrian
I grant you that. The whole Roman Empire witnesses to the serious troubles they can cause. The body politic is infected by the corpses of slaughtered Christians.

Antiochus
This woman of whom I speak is urging the people of this country to abandon the religion of their fathers and embrace the Christian faith.

Hadrian
But have her words any effect?

===Chinese===
安条克
扰乱太平。

哈德良
从何说起？

安条克
还有什么比宗教异见更能搅扰邦国的安宁与和睦呢？

哈德良
此点朕予承认。整个罗马帝国都可作证，它们能酿成何等严重的祸患。邦国之躯正被惨遭屠戮的基督徒的尸首所侵染。

安条克
臣所说的这个女子，正怂恿本国百姓抛弃祖先的宗教，皈依基督信仰。

哈德良
可她的话有人听吗？

===Original===
Antiochus
Indeed they have. Our wives hate and scorn us to such an extent that they will not deign to eat with us, still less share our beds.

Hadrian
This is a real danger, I admit.

Antiochus
You must protect yourself.

Hadrian
That stands to reason. Let the woman be brought before me, and I will examine her and see what can be done.

Antiochus
You wish me to summon her?

Hadrian
I have said it.

===Chinese===
安条克
确有效验。我们的妻子憎恶我们、鄙夷我们到了这般地步：不屑与我们一起进食，更不必说同床共枕。

哈德良
朕承认，这确是实祸。

安条克
陛下必须自保。

哈德良
此理自明。将那女子带到朕面前来，朕要亲自审问，再作处置。

安条克
陛下要臣去传唤她吗？

哈德良
朕已说过。

#### Scene II / 第二场

===Original===
Antiochus
Foreign woman, what is your name?

Sapientia
Sapientia.

Antiochus
The Emperor Hadrian orders you to present yourself at the palace.

Sapientia
I am not afraid to go. I have a noble escort in my daughters. Nor do I tremble at the thought of meeting your scowling Emperor face to face.

===Chinese===
安条克
外邦女子，你叫什么名字？

萨皮恩提亚（Sapientia）
我名叫萨皮恩提亚。

安条克
哈德良皇帝命你前往宫殿觐见。

萨皮恩提亚
我不惧前往。我有女儿们作高贵的扈从。想到要与你们那位怒容满面的皇帝当面相对，我也毫不动容。

===Original===
Antiochus
It is the way of you Christian rabble to defy authority.

Sapientia
We acknowledge the authority of Him Who rules the world; we know that He will not let His subjects be vanquished.

Antiochus
Not so much talk. To the palace.

Sapientia
Go before us and show the way. We will follow you.

===Chinese===
安条克
你们基督徒暴民就是这副德行，专会蔑视权威。

萨皮恩提亚
我们承认那统治世界者的权威；我们知道祂必不容祂的子民被征服。

安条克
休得多言。去宫殿。

萨皮恩提亚
你在前面引路吧。我们随你同去。

#### Scene III / 第三场

===Original===
Antiochus
That is the Emperor you see there, seated on his throne. Be careful what you say to him.

Sapientia
The word of Christ forbids us to take thought as to what we ought to say. His wisdom is sufficient for us.

Hadrian
Are you there, Antiochus?

Antiochus
At your service, my lord.

Hadrian
Are these the women whom you have arrested on account of their Christian opinions?

Antiochus
Yes, lord.

===Chinese===
安条克
那边你看见的便是皇帝，坐在御座上的就是。对他说话须小心。

萨皮恩提亚
基督的圣言不许我们预先思虑当说什么话；祂的智慧于我们已然足够。

哈德良
你在那边吗，安条克？

安条克
臣听候差遣，陛下。

哈德良
这些就是你因其基督徒见解而逮捕的女子吗？

安条克
正是，陛下。

===Original===
Hadrian
I am amazed at their beauty; I cannot help admiring their noble and dignified manner.

Antiochus
Waste no time in admiring them, my lord. Make them worship the gods.

Hadrian
It would be wiser to ask it as a favour to me at first. Then they may yield.

Antiochus
That may be best. This frail sex is easily moved by flattery.

Hadrian
Noble matron, if you desire to enjoy my friendship, I ask you in all gentleness to join me in an act of worship of the gods.

Sapientia
We have no desire for your friendship. And we refuse to worship your gods.

===Chinese===
哈德良
她们的美貌令朕惊叹；那高贵庄重的气度，令朕不能不心生赞赏。

安条克
陛下莫要在赞赏上虚耗光阴了。叫她们敬拜诸神吧。

哈德良
不如先以请托之姿，求她们看朕的情面。这样她们或许会屈从。

安条克
或许这样最好。这柔弱的性别最易被奉承打动。

哈德良
高贵的夫人，你若愿享受朕的友谊，朕以极尽温柔之恳求，请你与朕一同敬拜诸神。

萨皮恩提亚
我们对您的友谊毫无所求，也拒绝敬拜你们的神明。

===Original===
Hadrian
You will try in vain to rouse my anger. I feel no indignation against you. I appeal to you and your daughters as lovingly as if I were their own father.

Sapientia
My children are not to be cozened by such diabolical flattery. They scorn it as I do.

Faith
Yes, and laugh at it in our hearts.

Antiochus
What are you muttering there?

Sapientia
I was speaking to my daughters.

Hadrian
I judge from appearances that you are of noble race, but I would know more—to what country and family you belong, and your name.

===Chinese===
哈德良
你若想激怒朕，那是徒劳。朕对你并无愠怒。朕恳求你与你的女儿们，其情之切，宛如她们的生身父亲。

萨皮恩提亚
我的孩子们不会被这等魔鬼般的谄媚所诱骗。她们与我一样鄙视它。

信德（Faith）
是的，我们还在心里讥笑它呢。

安条克
你在那里咕哝什么？

萨皮恩提亚
我在同我的女儿们说话。

哈德良
观其仪表，朕断定你出身贵胄，但朕还想知道更多——你属于哪个国度、哪个家族，以及你的名字。

===Original===
Sapientia
Although we take no pride in it, I come of noble stock.

Hadrian
That is easy to believe.

Sapientia
My parents were princes of Greece, and I am called Sapientia.

Hadrian
The splendour of your ancestry is blazoned in your face, and the wisdom of your name sparkles on your lips.

Sapientia
You need not waste your breath in flattering us. We are not to be conquered by fair speeches.

Hadrian
Why have you left your own people and come to live here?

===Chinese===
萨皮恩提亚
虽然我们并不以此自负，我确出名门。

哈德良
这不难相信。

萨皮恩提亚
我的父母是希腊的君侯，我的名字叫萨皮恩提亚。

哈德良
你先祖的荣光昭然显在你脸上，你名字中的智慧在你唇边闪耀。

萨皮恩提亚
您不必枉费唇舌奉承我们。甜言蜜语征服不了我们。

哈德良
你为何离开自己的同胞，来此定居？

===Original===
Sapientia
For no other reason than that we wished to know the truth. I came to learn more of the faith which you persecute, and to consecrate my daughters to Christ.

Hadrian
Tell me their names.

Sapientia
